**(186) 1.1 SHIPPED, AND THE FIRST REAL ANDROID TESTER (2026-07-30, afternoon).** **1.1 IS LIVE** (READY_FOR_SALE, build 138 -- password reset, referrals, universal links, announcements, VS fixes). Then Doug (founder's brother-in-law, Galaxy S23 / **Android 16**) became the first person outside the team to install the Play build, and found in an hour what months of emulator checks had not.

**THE LESSON THAT MATTERS: THE EMULATOR WAS API 34.** targetSdk 35 opts the app into FORCED edge-to-edge on Android 15+ -- the window extends under the system bars and insetting becomes the app's job. On API 34 that enforcement does not exist, so EVERY verification run all session was structurally blind to it. On Doug's phone the gesture bar sat on top of the keyboard's bottom row (Z-M, backspace, ENTER: "makes submitting a guess very difficult") and the status bar sat on the corner Home/? buttons. Fixed in two passes -- nav bar app-wide at the root Surface (~20 screens shared the defect), status bar on the four full-screen game surfaces that hand-rolled 48/52/56dp top padding. A wrong first attempt is instructive: putting the inset on the scrolling COLUMN moved everything except the corner button, which is its SIBLING in the parent Box. Created an Android 16 AVD (Wordocious_A16), reproduced both bugs on it, then confirmed both fixes. **Verify Android on API 36 from now on.**

**GOOGLE SIGN-IN, THREE ROUNDS, STILL OPEN.** (a) "No credentials available" -- ruled out the usual cause (setFilterByAuthorizedAccounts was already false) and the consent screen (In production / External, so no test-user list). Registered both Play SHA-1s as Android OAuth clients; **the app-signing fingerprint recorded earlier in the session was WRONG** -- real value 5D:C5:F4:58..., read off the App signing page by intercepting the copy button. (b) Still failed ~1h40m later, which is long for propagation, so upgraded androidx.credentials 1.3.0 -> 1.6.0 (1.3.0 predates Android 15 AND 16) and added a GetSignInWithGoogleOption fallback when the One Tap sheet returns nothing. **The error WORDING is a version fingerprint** -- "No credentials available" is raw text only build 25 emits -- and that is how we proved Doug had never actually updated. (c) On 26+ the account picker appears (fallback works) but choosing an account does nothing. Prime suspect: GetCredentialCancellationException returns null by design for a real dismissal, and Play Services reports some genuine failures as cancellations -- indistinguishable, and it looks exactly like a dead button. Now reported to Sentry with the exception type. UNRESOLVED; needs Doug's next attempt.

**PLAY UPLOADS ARE NOW ONE COMMAND.** scripts/play-upload.mjs drives the Android Publisher API (service account play-publisher@spellstrike-490819, key at ~/.android-keys/play-publisher.json). Permissions deliberately testing-tracks-only, so production 403s and stays a deliberate click. Built because the browser file-upload tool caps at 10MB and the bundle is 22MB. Builds 25->29 shipped this way; internal testers list "Wordocious testers", opt-in https://play.google.com/apps/internaltest/4701001356292086063.

**THE API-KEY SCARE'S REAL FINDING.** GitHub flagged the Google API key in the committed google-services.json. That alert is noise by design -- the file ships inside every APK. But chasing it found both Firebase-created keys had Application restrictions = NONE on a PUBLIC repo. Android key now restricted to com.wordocious.app + all three SHA-1s (app signing, upload, AND debug -- miss the debug one and local builds break); browser key restricted to wordocious.com. **When a scanner flags a public-by-design credential, the alert is noise but the audit it triggers may not be.**

**"HYMEN" WAS THE WORD OF THE DAY**, printed on every home screen with the full anatomical definition, seen by the tester. Fixed with a DISPLAY-ONLY blocklist (packages/core/src/wotd-blocklist.ts): the word stays in solutions.json because removing it would shift every later day's index and rewrite played history -- what SOLUTIONS_CUTOVER_DATE exists to prevent -- and is simply skipped for featuring, like a word with no definition. 18 of 27 crude matches survived inspection by reading the DEFINITION THAT RENDERS: COLON is punctuation, STOOL is a seat, SHOOT is a plant stem. BLOOD is blocked for an unguessable reason -- its first sense in our dataset is "A member of the Los Angeles gang The Bloods." Four call sites re-implement the candidate walk (web server, web home card, iOS, Android); a test fails if any drifts.

**THE FIXED-SURFACE / THEMED-INK BUG CLASS.** The dark keyboard was unreadable on ALL THREE platforms (recorded as two): the key SURFACE is a fixed brand #E8E5F0 that ignores the theme, but the letter used the THEMED text color -- #F0EEF6 in Dark, measured at **1.08:1** against WCAG AA's 4.5. Fixed ink #1A1A2E is 13.72:1. Worth generalizing: **a fixed background demands a fixed foreground.** Also of note, the first web fix only changed ENTER/backspace and was reported as done while A-Z were still invisible -- check every variant.

**HEADER POP-IN, SAME SHAPE AS THE PRO CACHE.** Streak/shield pills were gated on `profile`, which arrives a beat after launch, so the header visibly assembled itself every cold start. Both now paint from cached values during that window. Presence must be stored distinctly from zero (nil / -1, not `integer(forKey:)`'s silent 0) or a first launch draws a phantom "0". **Android had no Pro cache at all** -- iOS got one 2026-07-29 and the port never happened, so Android flickered the PRO badge too; it cached the streak but only NotificationService read it.

**ADS: SKIPPABLE NOW, BUT THE CLOSE BUTTON IS GOOGLE'S.** The rewarded->standard interstitial swap works (a X appears). Its contrast is AdMob's rendering and there is no API to restyle it; the only lever would be dropping interstitials entirely.

**ADMIN GIFT PRO.** The founder wanted to comp friends. The machinery existed (/admin/users/[id], verifyAdmin, audit log) but the grant computed `now + days` FLAT under a button labelled "Extend Pro" -- comping 7 days to an annual subscriber would have cut them to 7. Now stacks via Math.max like the Stripe path; added a one-click "Gift 7 days (free)" matching the referral trial. Costs nothing: it writes profiles.pro_expires_at with the service role, and entitlement is one row all three clients read, so a gift lands without a build.

**STORE METADATA IS FROZEN WHILE LIVE.** Subtitle and screenshots both 409 INVALID_STATE against a READY_FOR_SALE version. Creating the 1.2 record spawned an editable appInfo and both writes went straight through: subtitle "Epic Word Battles" (it was EMPTY, which is why the App Store printed the category "Word" under the name) and the 6.5" set reordered so the Go Pro paywall is last instead of first. Neither is public until 1.2 ships. **Do listing edits in the PREPARE_FOR_SUBMISSION window, first thing.**

**(185) PLAY LAUNCH INFRA + THE API-KEY SCARE (2026-07-30).** Four things, none of which live in code. (a) ADS: the game-start interstitial was a REWARDED interstitial on both platforms — the rewarded contract makes AdMob hold the close button for the full ~30s because the user is nominally earning something, and we granted nothing, so free players sat through every ad with no exit (founder hit this testing with Pro off). Swapped both to plain interstitials (iOS unit .../1609544807, Android .../8174953152); ad format is fixed at unit creation, hence new units. ADMOB_SETUP.md had documented the rewarded choice as deliberate — corrected, or someone switches it back. (b) PLAY: internal-testing release 25 (1.0) published, tester list "Wordocious testers" attached, opt-in link https://play.google.com/apps/internaltest/4701001356292086063. Uploads are no longer drag-and-drop — scripts/play-upload.mjs drives the Android Publisher API (service account play-publisher@spellstrike-490819, key at ~/.android-keys/play-publisher.json, permissions deliberately TESTING-TRACKS-ONLY so production is a 403 and stays a deliberate click). Browser file upload caps at 10MB and the bundle is 21MB, which is why this had to exist. (c) OAUTH: both Play SHA-1s registered as Android clients — app signing 5D:C5:F4:58..., upload 1F:13:CB:E5.... The value carried in earlier notes as the app-signing fingerprint (04:8B:0C:8C...) was WRONG; the real one came off the App signing page. First tester still got "No credentials available" ~30min later — most likely propagation (Google says up to hours), and AuthService now falls back from the One Tap sheet to GetSignInWithGoogleOption, which also dodges the 24h dismissal cooldown. UNPROVEN until a real device signs in. (d) GITHUB flagged the Google API key in the committed google-services.json. That alert is a FALSE ALARM by design — google-services.json ships inside every APK, the key identifies the project rather than authenticating anyone, and it is not rotatable-worthy. But chasing it found the real problem: both Firebase-created API keys had Application restrictions = NONE on a PUBLIC repo, so anyone could spend our quota across the 25 enabled APIs. Android key now restricted to com.wordocious.app + all three SHA-1s (app signing, upload, AND debug — miss the debug one and local builds break); browser key (unused — nothing in apps/web imports Firebase) restricted to wordocious.com. Lesson: when a scanner flags a public-by-design credential, the alert is noise but the audit it triggers may not be.

A complete walkthrough of what Wordocious is, how it's built, where it stands, and what's pending — written for someone joining on the business side.

---

## 1. The product in one sentence

Wordocious is a daily word-puzzle game (Wordle's lineage, expanded) with ~9 distinct modes — solo dailies, multi-board variants, a 5-stage Gauntlet, and real-time head-to-head VS matches — live on the web (wordocious.com), **iOS v1.0 APPROVED by App Review and held UNRELEASED (manual release)** while the developer iterates on TestFlight — **build 127 is the latest** (as of 2026-07-20; the detailed newest-first running log lives in the header changelog above, builds 100+) — and feature-complete as a native Android app awaiting Play Store setup (target API 35; signed AAB + Play developer account pending). Because iOS is still pre-release, content/answer changes roll out immediately with no installed-base migration concern.

## 2. Why it exists

Wordle proved a single daily 5-letter puzzle could become a habit. Wordocious takes that loop and multiplies it: multiple board counts (1/2/4/8), letter lengths (5/6/7), a roguelike Gauntlet that chains modes into one run, and synchronous multiplayer where two players race the same seed. Daily seeds mean everyone plays the same puzzle each day — the shared-water-cooler effect that made Wordle viral — while the mode variety and VS ladder give committed players somewhere to go after the daily.

Monetization is two-pronged: a **Pro** subscription (unlimited replays, streak shields, **ad-free**, etc.) and **ads** (AdMob on iOS and Android / AdSense on web) shown to free users. Neither gates the core daily — it stays free to protect the habit loop. See §5.

---

## 3. Technical stack

| Layer | Stack |
|---|---|
| Web app | Next.js 13 (App Router), React 18, TypeScript, Tailwind + shadcn/ui. Full SSR (API routes + middleware) — **not** a static export. |
| Native iOS | 100% native SwiftUI (no Capacitor/WebView). Game engine is a Swift port of `packages/core`; data via `supabase-swift`; realtime VS live on `socket.io-client-swift`; IAP live on StoreKit 2. |
| Native Android | 100% native Kotlin + Jetpack Compose (`apps/android`, `:app` + `:core` Gradle modules; minSdk 26 / target 34). Game engine is a Kotlin port of `packages/core` in `:core`; data via supabase-kt; realtime VS on `io.socket:socket.io-client`; IAP via Google Play Billing (billing-ktx 7.1.1); ads via AdMob + UMP; daily reminder notifications via WorkManager (18:00 local). |
| Realtime server | Standalone `socket.io` server (`apps/server`) on **Railway** behind `server.wordocious.com` (deploys from `main`) — matchmaking (bucketed by mode + dailySeed\|random) + live VS match state. |
| Shared engine | `packages/core` — pure TypeScript game logic (evaluator, reducer, seed, scoring, dictionary, prefill). Source of truth for game rules across web + the Swift (iOS) and Kotlin (Android) ports. |
| Hosting | Vercel (web, wordocious.com — the live Vercel project is `wordocious`, renamed from `spellstrike` 2026-07-20) + Railway (socket server). DNS at GoDaddy. Both deploy from `main`. |
| Auth / DB / Storage | Supabase (Postgres + Auth + Storage). Project `eniiqqsxpmuyrspvepiw`. RLS enabled + correct on all 13 public tables. Email + Google + Apple sign-in all functional on iOS (confirmed on device). Android Google sign-in is built (Credential Manager) but blocked on creating an **Android OAuth client** in Google Cloud (package `com.wordocious.app` + SHA-1 `7A:2C:E8:AC:82:B7:F7:F6:10:64:E1:9D:CC:88:23:0A:8E:63:79:B0`) before tokens vend. |
| Payments (web) | **LIVE: Stripe Checkout** via the `lib/payment/` abstraction (PaymentProvider interface + factory → `StripeProvider`). Dedicated **Wordocious Stripe account** (`acct_1Tvk33BSPueCC99p`, separate account under the ShowLoud, LLC login). Server-authoritative: signed webhook grants/revokes Pro via service-role; Customer Portal for self-serve cancel. Full lifecycle validated end-to-end in test mode 2026-07-21 (entry 133). |
| Payments (iOS) | **Live: StoreKit 2 direct** (`com.wordocious.app.pro_{monthly,yearly,day}`); purchases tag `appAccountToken` = the Supabase user id. The App Store Server Notifications V2 webhook (`apps/web/app/api/appstore/notifications/route.ts`) is **REGISTERED** (prod + sandbox), a Day-Pass server-verify endpoint (`/api/appstore/verify-transaction`) handles the consumable Apple never notifies about, and the **Pro-columns lock is APPLIED** (admin-gated trigger, 2026-07-20) — entitlement writes are server-authoritative (see §11). |
| Payments (Android) | Google Play Billing (billing-ktx 7.1.1): `pro_monthly`/`pro_yearly` subs (+4 shields) + stacking `pro_day` consumable, `obfuscatedAccountId` = user id, restore supported. The 3 products still need to be **created in Play Console**. A Play RTDN (Real-Time Developer Notifications) webhook (`apps/web/app/api/playstore/notifications/route.ts`) is built fail-closed (mirrors the Apple one) but **not yet configured** (see §11). |
| Cron | Vercel cron: `/api/cron/daily-medals` (12:05 UTC — moved from 05:05 so US-evening local-day rows are finalized before podium grading), `/api/cron/daily-reminder` (14:00 UTC), `/api/cron/expire-pro` (08:00 UTC — daily sweep flipping `is_pro` off past `pro_expires_at`; **Hobby plan caps crons at once/day** — anything more frequent silently fails every deploy). Auth via `CRON_SECRET`. |
| iOS project gen | XcodeGen (`project.yml` → `Wordocious.xcodeproj`, gitignored & regenerated). |
| Android build | Gradle: `./gradlew bundleRelease` produces a **signed release AAB (~18.5MB)** via the gitignored `apps/android/key.properties` → upload keystore at `~/.android-keys/wordocious-upload.jks` (**must be backed up**). |

---

## 4. Architecture: three clients, one backend

The web app and the native iOS and Android apps are **three separate clients that share one backend** (Supabase + the socket server). Neither native app is a WebView shell — each renders fully native UI and runs its own port of the game engine. Nothing about adding a native client changes the web app.

| Concern | Web | iOS (native) | Android (native) |
|---|---|---|---|
| UI | React / Tailwind | SwiftUI | Jetpack Compose |
| Game logic | `packages/core` (TS) | Swift port of `packages/core` (parity-tested) | Kotlin port of `packages/core` in `:core` (parity-tested) |
| Auth | `supabase-js` + RLS | `supabase-swift` + RLS (same `auth.uid()`) | supabase-kt + RLS (same `auth.uid()`) |
| Data | Direct Supabase reads + Next API routes | Direct Supabase reads | Direct Supabase reads |
| Realtime VS | `socket.io-client` | `socket.io-client-swift` (live) | `io.socket:socket.io-client` (live) |
| Pro entitlement | `is_pro` + `pro_expires_at`, read via `isProActive()` | same columns, `isProActive()` ported 1:1 | same columns, ported 1:1 |
| Ads | AdSense | AdMob (real IDs, approved) | AdMob (Google **test IDs** until an Android AdMob app exists) |
| IAP | Stripe Checkout (LIVE) | StoreKit 2 (live) | Google Play Billing (built; products pending in Play Console) |

**Engine parity is the load-bearing constraint.** Because daily seeds and VS matches must resolve to the *same words* on every client, the Swift and Kotlin engines are 1:1 ports validated against JSON fixtures generated from the live TS engine (see §8). Any two players on the same daily seed — web, iOS, or Android — get the same puzzle, byte-for-byte.

**Why fully native (not a Capacitor shell):** the sibling app ShowLoud was repeatedly rejected by Apple under Guideline 4.2 ("minimum functionality") while it was a WebView wrapper; rejections stopped once it went fully native. Wordocious — an even more web-shaped SSR app — would hit the same wall, so it's native from day one. (Validated: build 10 passed App Review.)

### Game modes

| Mode | Boards | Guesses | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| DUEL (Classic) | 1 | 6 | 5-letter daily |
| DUEL_6 / DUEL_7 (Six / Seven) | 1 | 7 / 8 | 6- and 7-letter, separate dictionaries |
| MULTI_DUEL | 2 | 6 | |
| QUORDLE (QuadWord) | 4 | 9 | |
| OCTORDLE (OctoWord) | 8 | 13 | |
| SEQUENCE | 4 | 10 | boards solved in order |
| RESCUE (Deliverance) | 4 | 6 | starts with 3 prefilled guesses |
| TOURNAMENT | 5 | 6 | |
| PROPERNOUNDLE | 1 | — | proper-noun puzzles, separate data |
| GAUNTLET | 1→4→4→4→8 | per-stage | 5-stage run chaining Duel→Quordle→Sequence→Rescue→Octordle |

---

## 5. Monetization

Two channels: **(A) Pro subscription** (removes ads + unlocks extras) and **(B) ads** shown to free users. The core daily stays free either way.

### A. Pro subscription

**Three plans** (defined in `lib/payment/types.ts` `PRO_PLANS`):
- `pro_day` — $1.00 / 24 hours (day pass; stacks on existing expiry, no streak-shield grant)
- `pro_monthly` — $6.99 / month
- `pro_yearly` — $59.99 / year (~$4.99/mo)

**Unified entitlement.** All Pro state lives in two `profiles` columns: `is_pro` (write-side marker) and `pro_expires_at`. Every Pro gate reads through `isProActive(profile)` — never the raw boolean (belt-and-suspenders: a daily `expire-pro` cron also sweeps `is_pro` off past-expiry rows, but the read path never trusts the boolean alone). Monthly/yearly grant 4 streak shields; the day pass intentionally does not.

**The swap point.** `lib/payment/` is a clean abstraction: a `PaymentProvider` interface and a factory in `index.ts` ("the single swap point for monetization"). The factory returns **`StripeProvider`** — real Stripe Checkout sessions; fulfillment happens ONLY server-side in the signed webhook (`app/api/stripe/webhook` → `stripe-fulfillment.ts`, service-role writes, idempotent per event id). Subscription shields (+4/period) are granted solely by `invoice.paid` — first period AND renewals — never by the checkout event (the double-grant bug killed in entry 133). **Changing prices later is routine**: Stripe prices are immutable, so a change = create a new price on the same product + swap the env var + update the display strings; existing subscribers keep their old price (grandfathered) unless deliberately migrated.

**Billing status by platform:**
- **iOS — LIVE: StoreKit 2 direct, server-authoritative.** Product IDs `com.wordocious.app.pro_{monthly,yearly,day}` (day pass = consumable that stacks 24h on the existing expiry); purchases tag `.appAccountToken(user id)`. **Hardening is now ENFORCED (2026-07-20/21):** the App Store Server Notifications V2 webhook is REGISTERED (prod + sandbox; verifies Apple's signed payload, maps `appAccountToken`→user, service-role writes); a **Day-Pass verify endpoint** (`/api/appstore/verify-transaction`, client posts the transaction JWS) covers the consumable Apple doesn't reliably notify about — validated end-to-end in sandbox; subs route through it too for instant entitlement; and the **Pro-columns lock is APPLIED** (`protect_pro_columns` trigger, admin-gated: regular clients can no longer write `is_pro`/`pro_expires_at`/`is_admin`/`is_banned`; admins keep the in-app Simulate Pro dev toggle; `streak_shields` deliberately stays client-writable as a game mechanic — mobile CLIENTS grant renewal shields, store webhooks grant none, Stripe grants server-side on web). Restore Purchases ships (Apple-mandatory). Apple takes 15–30% of iOS subscription revenue (15% at our size via the Small Business Program).
- **Android — built, pending store setup: Google Play Billing** (billing-ktx 7.1.1). `StoreManager` mirrors iOS: `pro_monthly`/`pro_yearly` subs (+4 shields), stacking `pro_day` consumable, `obfuscatedAccountId` = user id, restore. Pro-page CTAs show real prices with fallbacks. **User TODO: create the 3 products in Play Console** + an internal-testing track + license testers.
- **Web — LIVE: Stripe** on the dedicated Wordocious account (`acct_1Tvk33BSPueCC99p` — a separate Stripe account under the same ShowLoud, LLC login: own statement descriptor, webhook isolation, clean books; created 2026-07-21). 3 live products (see §10); checkout → signed webhook grant → portal cancel → revoke all validated end-to-end in test mode first (entry 133 — the validation caught 4 real bugs before any customer could). Stripe takes ~2.9% + 30¢, so the web nets ~97% vs ~85% through the app stores — same Pro, better margin, and we own the customer/refund relationship.

### B. Ads (free users only)

Free users see ads; **Pro removes them entirely** (every ad surface is gated on `isProActive`). The serving format on native is a **game-start rewarded interstitial** (banner units exist on iOS but are unused — interstitial-only is full parity across native); the web has an interstitial-on-game-start + bottom banner.

- **iOS — AdMob** (`GoogleMobileAds` SDK, `AdsManager`/`AdBannerView`). **Account + app approved by Google.** Real units under publisher **`pub-3015627373086578`**: App ID `…~8393761846`, banner `…/4287985559`, rewarded-interstitial `…/6909445311`. Launch flow on first play: **Google UMP consent** (GDPR, EEA/UK) → **ATT** prompt (hardened `requestATTOnce`, see §11) → SDK init → ads serve (gated on `canRequestAds`). The **GDPR (UMP) consent message is PUBLISHED in the AdMob console** (2026-06-10; privacy policy URL `https://wordocious.com/privacy` set). `app-ads.txt` (`google.com, pub-3015627373086578, DIRECT, f08c47fec0942fa0`) is live at wordocious.com. **Remaining:** payment profile (banking), then link the App Store listing + verify app-ads.txt once the app is publicly released.
- **Android — AdMob wired but on Google TEST IDs.** UMP consent → MobileAds init → game-start rewarded interstitial for free users (ad time excluded from the game timer). **User TODO: create a Wordocious ANDROID app in the AdMob console and swap the real IDs** into `AndroidManifest` (APPLICATION_ID) + `AdsManager.kt`.
- **Web — AdSense** (`pub-3015627373086578`, same publisher). Interstitial-on-game-start (`AdGate`) + bottom `AdBanner`; `ads.txt` present. Activation is config (`NEXT_PUBLIC_ADS_ENABLED` + slot IDs) + site approval. **DENIED 2026-06-13 for "Low value content"** — cause is structural (gameplay is login-gated, so the crawler saw only thin public pages). Remediated same day: 9 substantive public mode guides at `/guides/*` (rules, real scoring formulas, strategy) + landing/sitemap wiring. **User TODO: request AdSense re-review** after the ~48h crawl window. (AdMob app ads are a separate program and unaffected.)
- AdMob takes a revenue share (publisher keeps ~68% on content ads).

## 6. Core user flows

### 6a-0. First run — NO ACCOUNT REQUIRED (guest mode)

**Anyone can play without an account, on all three platforms** — shipped 2026-06-17
(builds 33/34) as the fix for App Review rejection 5.1.1(v) (forced registration
before single-player play), and it's why v1.0 passed review. Code-verified state:

- **Guests can:** play every solo mode + daily (web `AuthGate` passes `isGuest`
  through to gameplay; both natives mirror it).
- **Guests can't:** play VS (account-based — live opponents + recorded results;
  a "Sign in to play VS" card explains why), or persist anything — no streaks,
  leaderboard identity, medals, stats, or cross-device sync (all reads/writes
  key on `auth.uid()`).
- **Conversion surface:** a persistent header **Sign In** button (web + both
  natives, build 34) and the sign-in prompts on the retention features guests
  bump into. The account ask lands AFTER the hook, not before puzzle one.
- **Known routing gap (open decision, distribution memo Part 2):** share/invite
  links land on the LANDING page where the visitor must choose guest — not
  directly on the board. "Frictionless first tap" (auto-guest into gameplay for
  share/VS-invite links) is a UX routing change, not a feature build.

### 6a. Play the daily (solo)
1. Open the app → pick a mode from the home grid.
2. Today's puzzle loads from the **daily seed** (`daily-YYYY-MM-DD-<MODE>`, keyed to the device-local date) — identical on web, iOS, and Android.
3. Type guesses on the on-screen keyboard; tiles reveal the **Royal palette** — purple (correct) / amber (present) / slate (absent); the keyboard keys color to the best-known state per letter. (Colorblind mode swaps to orange/blue; the palette is purely client-side, no server/DB color storage.)
4. Win/loss is computed client-side by the engine; result persists locally (resume mid-game after backgrounding) and to Supabase for stats/leaderboards.

### 6b. Gauntlet
A single run through 5 escalating stages (Duel → Quordle → Sequence → Rescue → Octordle). Any board bust ends the run; clearing a stage advances to the next. Results screen shows a per-stage breakdown with a "review this stage" snapshot.

### 6c. VS (head-to-head)
Two players are matched via the socket server — **matchmaking is bucketed by (mode, dailySeed|random)** so clients only pair on the same puzzle — share a seed, and race. Live on all three clients. The 2026-06-10 overhaul added: a match-intro splash with all-time head-to-head record, a persistent tug-of-war progress header, moment callouts + typing indicator, a spectator waiting screen for the finished player, and a side-by-side final-board reveal (letters only after match end). Scoring = win bonus + guess diff + time diff + DNF penalty via `calculateScore`; rematch flow included. Daily-VS uses a shared daily seed; private matches use an invite code. **ALL completed VS matches now count on the daily VS Records leaderboard**, and the personal profile has a Solo/VS toggle. *(Pending: a live cross-platform pairing test of the bucketing fix.)*

### 6d. Go Pro
On `/pro` (web) the plans + copy are shown; the Subscribe button is what branches by platform. **Web runs live Stripe Checkout** (redirect → pay → the webhook grants Pro server-side → return to `/pro?purchase=success`); **iOS runs live StoreKit 2**; **Android runs Google Play Billing** (live once the products exist in Play Console). Cancel paths: web = settings → "Manage web subscription" → Stripe Customer Portal; mobile = the store's own manage-subscription surface (all three linked in settings). Entitlement lights up everywhere via `isProActive()`.

### Build & change log (newest first)

*2026-06-15 part 2: iOS build 22 VALID on TestFlight (Delivery f68a16bd). Android Daily Reminders toggle added to the profile Account section — the notification BACKEND already existed (NotificationService.kt WorkManager chain + a working Settings toggle); the profile-audit agent had wrongly reported "no backend" and omitted it. Now wired into the profile via the existing service (web/iOS parity).*

*2026-06-15 (FULL PROFILE PARITY AUDIT + swipe-back → iOS build 22; web deployed earlier): user reported build 21 STILL showed no Pro Stats + missing stats vs web, and wanted a full profile parity pass (web = truth) on both natives. Two parallel audit agents (iOS, Android) reconciled every section/stat/chart/menu against apps/web/app/profile + components/profile. **REAL Pro Stats root cause (the bug my null-decode fix didn't catch):** iOS ProStatsCard self-fetched via .task(id: auth.isProActive) — a Bool id — so when auth.profile hydrated a frame late the guard early-returned and the task NEVER re-fired → bars empty forever → card permanently hidden for a Pro user. Fixed by fetching statRows once in ProfileTab (.task id = profile.id) and passing down; bars is now a synchronous computed property. **iOS profile gaps fixed:** reordered All view to web order (Activity → Last 7 Days → Guess Dist → Solve Time → Top Words → Insights → Daily Medals → Pro Stats; medals were rendering at the very top); ADDED the missing Last 7 Days chart + Insights card; ADDED an Account section (Daily Reminders toggle, Sign Out, Delete Account) with bottom padding so Sign Out clears the BottomNav/safe area (the unreachable-sign-out bug). **Android profile gaps fixed:** Delete Account added to the profile (was Settings-only); Sign Out restyled + navigationBarsPadding so it clears the gesture nav; Last 7 Days now All-view-only + global; When You Play now per-mode-only; activity() counts player1 OR player2, gapless (web parity). Pro Stats solo-filter parity retained on both. **Swipe-back-to-home:** new iOS SwipeToGoBack.swift modifier — left-edge DragGesture via simultaneousGesture (won't block keyboard/tile taps) on GameScreen (all modes + post-game), ProperNoundleView, SolvedPuzzleView; dismiss() covers both fullScreenCover + pushed presentations. Live VS excluded (its exit forfeits). Android already had full BackHandler coverage = system edge-swipe back works everywhere. Build 22 carries all of it + everything from build 21; Android changes committed (AAB on request). Note: web Account section has a NotificationToggle Android still lacks a backend for — omitted, noted for later. The build-swap onto the approved 1.0 version still must be done in the ASC web UI (Pending-Developer-Release API limit, §9/§12) — build 22 is now the swap candidate once blessed.*

*2026-06-13 part 3 (post-build-20 polish wave → iOS build 21; web deployed): a run of user-reported fixes across all 3 platforms. **AdSense guides were invisible to crawlers** — AuthGate's PUBLIC_PATHS omitted /guides, so signed-out visitors + Google's JS-rendering crawler got the login Landing at every /guides URL (the guide content only existed in pre-hydration HTML). Added /guides to PUBLIC_PATHS — the remediation guides now actually render. Also gave the landing + guide pages real game icons (were letter/roman placeholders). **Solved-board ✓ badge clipped** on right-column boards (offset overhang hit the ScrollView clip bound) → anchored flush, kept vertical float. **Completed-daily card cross-device**: web (non-gauntlet) + Android only read the local session, so a daily beaten on another device showed nothing; added the server replay fallback (replayRecordedGuesses) on both — iOS already had it. Native summary wording corrected to web parity ("4/6 · 13s" single, "solved/total · Ng · 13s" multi) — the leaderboard ROW stays "4 Guesses · 13s". **Web profile**: Pro Stats showed raw DUEL_6/DUEL_7 (MODE_LABELS missing → now Six/Seven); sign-out cleared the footer (pb-20→pb-32). **iOS Pro Stats showed nothing even on the All view (real bug)**: UserStatRow decoded best_score/average_time/fastest_time as non-optional Int, but those are NULL for some rows (post-reconcile / no recorded time); Swift's strict JSONDecoder threw and the `try?` collapsed the whole fetch to [] → empty bars. Fixed with a null-tolerant custom init (decodeIfPresent ?? 0). Android was immune (Supabase serializer coerceInputValues=true). Both natives also now solo-filter Pro Stats to match web. Web fixes deployed; iOS fixes ride in **build 21** (also carries everything from build 20). Android changes committed (next AAB on request). Earlier this session: build-20 ASC swap is BLOCKED by the Pending-Developer-Release API limitation — must be done in the ASC web UI (see §9/§12); build 21 will be the one to swap once blessed.*

*2026-06-13 part 2 (build-20 swap ATTEMPTED, blocked by ASC API — NOT released): user asked to swap build 20 onto the approved 1.0 version (manual release). Build 20 is VALID on TestFlight. The swap could NOT be completed via the ASC API: version 1.0 is in PENDING_DEVELOPER_RELEASE (build 10 approved), and the API refuses to change the build relationship (INVALID_STATE) or add the version to a new reviewSubmission (STATE_NOT_SUITABLE_TO_SUBMIT: "Pending Developer Release") — there is no API endpoint to developer-reject an approved version back to editable. This is a NEW finding: the cancel→patch-build→resubmit flow only works from IN_REVIEW/WAITING, not from an approved-pending-release version. NOTHING BROKEN: build 10 still approved + releasable, release still MANUAL. The swap must be done in the ASC web UI (replace the build on the version, which performs the implicit un-approval) — steps in §12. One inert empty draft reviewSubmission (a176542b, 0 items, unsubmitted) remains as a harmless artifact (API allows neither DELETE nor cancel on an unsubmitted empty one). Durable ASC helper added at ~/.appstoreconnect/asc.rb (the /tmp scripts had been cleaned out, same as the export plist).*

*2026-06-13 (iOS build 20 — audit waves 1-3 + Royal palette; Delivery 41a28877): cut build 20 carrying everything since build 19 (PN daily-seed leak fix, POSIX date formatters, sequence guess-list shape + reconstruction, day-from-seed recording, gauntlet guess-list parity, UTC activity-calendar bucketing, PN Wikipedia clue consumes a board row + can lose, PN vowel/consonant loss check + reducer fix, Six/Seven zero-candidate hint counting). BUILD-PIPELINE NOTE: /tmp/wr_export.plist had been cleaned out of /tmp; recreating it by hand surfaced two gotchas now documented — the export plist MUST use signingStyle=automatic (with -allowProvisioningUpdates + the API key, so xcodebuild fetches the App Store provisioning profile; manual+no-profile produces an IPA with no embedded.mobileprovision → 409), and the applesignin re-sign MUST dump the COMPLETE exported entitlements first (codesign -d --entitlements - --xml, which includes application-identifier + team-identifier) then add applesignin — a partial/stale ent.plist drops application-identifier → 409. Working plist backed up to ~/.appstoreconnect/wr_export.plist so /tmp cleanup can't recur. Awaiting VALID on TestFlight.*

STILL OPEN from audits (wave 3 backlog): Android has NO sweep/flawless bonuses, streak/perfect medals, or achievement unlock detection (feature port or move server-side); systemic lost-result risk (terminal state persists locally before the fire-and-forget network record on ALL platforms — needs a persisted pending-record retry queue); Android game timer counts backgrounded time (needs the web/iOS accumulator); daily day-from-seed at midnight rollover (all 3 record onto the finish-time date); PN Wikipedia-clue consumes a board row on web but not natives; Six/Seven zero-candidate hint counted on web only; Android reminder drifts after DST; Android test AdMob IDs + no banner (known user-side AdMob setup); WEB DECISION NEEDED: non-daily game routes (/quordle etc., linked from share pages) have no Pro gate — free users get unlimited play via URL; no Google Play RTDN webhook (blocks lock_pro_columns migration without breaking Android purchases). iOS builds 18 (Royal) + 19 (Royal + guess-count/keyboard/gauntlet fixes) VALID on TestFlight; wave 1+2 iOS fixes await the next build cut.*

*2026-06-09 update (iOS App Store REJECTION fix → build 9 uploaded; macOS iCloud build-hang root-caused & fixed): **App Review rejected build 8 (1.0)** on two guidelines (reviewed on iPad Air 5): **(3.1.2(c) Subscriptions/EULA)** the App Store Description lacked a functional Terms of Use (EULA) link. FIX (metadata, no build needed — done in ASC): appended a WORDOCIOUS PRO section to the Description with subscription names/lengths/prices (Monthly $6.99, Yearly $59.99), standard auto-renewal language, a link to the standard Apple EULA (https://www.apple.com/legal/internet-services/itunes/dev/stdeula/), and Privacy Policy (https://wordocious.com/privacy); Saved. **(5.1.2(i) Privacy/ATT)** privacy labels declare tracking but the reviewer never saw the ATT prompt. ROOT CAUSE: ATT was requested at the tail of a fragile chain (cold-launch `.task` → Google UMP network round-trip → consent form → ATT) AND while the app could still be `.inactive` (iOS silently returns `.denied` WITHOUT showing the prompt). FIX in `AdsManager.swift` (committed 9ebbbca): new `requestATTOnce()` that presents the prompt exactly once but only once `UIApplication.applicationState == .active` (retries every 0.4s until active), plus a 3s watchdog so ATT fires even if the UMP callback never returns (offline during review); `afterConsent` routes through it before GMA init. Personalized ads retained (user chose keep-ads over non-tracking). Bumped CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION 8→9. Added ATT-location + EULA Review Notes in ASC. **Build 9 cut WITHOUT the cloud re-sign quirk:** archived UNSIGNED (`CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO`) → `xcodebuild -exportArchive` (app-store-connect, automatic signing, `-allowProvisioningUpdates` + ASC API key C8FRS9T697 / issuer 8bdd3f73-0d8b-427d-95c7-8097b77dfb7a — App Store distribution profiles need NO registered devices, unlike the dev profile that blocked a direct signed archive). Export STILL stripped `com.apple.developer.applesignin` (known quirk) → re-signed the .app with full entitlements incl applesignin (embedded `iOS Team Store Provisioning Profile` permits it) → repackaged → `altool --upload-app` SUCCEEDED (Delivery UUID 384f3c0b). Build 9 processing in ASC. **NEXT:** once processed, attach build 9 to the 1.0 version (replace build 8), confirm Manual release, Resubmit to App Review. **macOS BUILD-HANG ROOT CAUSE (also hits ShowLoud):** repos lived under `~/Desktop` which is iCloud-synced ("Desktop & Documents" + "Optimize Mac Storage" ON); at 91% disk, iCloud evicted project file CONTENTS (names stay, so `ls`/`find` look fine but reads hang forever), and `fileproviderd` pegged 130%+ CPU. Fixed: cleared 13GB (DerivedData 5.4G + old Archives 7.7G), killed wedged git/xcodebuild procs, and **cloned a fresh fully-local copy to `~/Developer/WordRush`** (reads went minutes→0.009s) — all iOS build work now runs there. RECOMMEND to user: turn OFF iCloud "Optimize Mac Storage" (do NOT toggle "Desktop & Documents" off — current macOS removes local files), and keep code under `~/Developer` (non-synced). Stale committed `apps/ios/build/` dir flagged for cleanup. Active git repo is now BOTH the old ~/Desktop copy and ~/Developer clone (same origin); committing from ~/Developer going forward.***

Added is_admin to Profile. Verified live: flips AppDevTester to PRO (header/pill badges), enabling the Pro-only Home UI. **(#90 no-shift)** All home heroes now share a fixed `HERO_HEIGHT` (78dp, centered) so toggling Daily<->Unlimited no longer shifts the game-cards grid — VERIFIED LIVE: GAME MODES y=887 / Classic y=1103 identical in BOTH states (was a ~10px jump). **(#90 Sweep/Flawless)** DailyHero now swaps to **Daily Sweep!** (purple/AutoAwesome, "All 9 dailies completed · +200 XP earned") once all 9 dailies are done, and **Flawless Victory!** (gold/EmojiEvents, "+600 XP earned") when all 9 are won — matching web page.tsx; the Profile Today's-Dailies card already had these states. **(Pro Unlimited UI — verified live via Simulate Pro)** Daily/Unlimited PlayModeToggle, UnlimitedHero, and the teal VS-swords overlay on non-VS cards all render correctly for Pro. **WEB BUG FOUND (diagnosed via the user's Chrome + Supabase):** the web's Simulate Pro button only flips is_pro and never touches pro_expires_at, so once an account's pro_expires_at lapses, toggling is_pro can't re-activate Pro (isProActive = is_pro && (expires_at null || > now)). BMT had pro_expires_at=2026-06-06 (expired) → not Pro despite is_pro=true; fixed by setting pro_expires_at=null. RECOMMEND patching web Simulate Pro (+ Android setProDev) to also clear/extend pro_expires_at. **Remaining Android: #80 VS 2-player test, #75 full mode playthrough, Google Play Billing; optional: harden Simulate-Pro to clear pro_expires_at.***

*2026-06-08 update (Android parity sweep, part 4 — #88 XP pipeline + toast + onboarding, XP path verified live): Android previously NEVER recorded games (the Profile charts were fed only by web/iOS plays of the same account) — closed that gap. New **GameResultsService.kt** ports iOS GameResultsService 1:1: on a LIVE finish it writes a `matches` history row (powers the charts), upserts the `user_stats` aggregate (wins/losses/games/best/avg/fastest), and updates `profiles` progression — XP (+100 win / +25 loss, +50 win-streak>1, +50 daily), level (xp/1000+1), win-streak, daily-login-streak (+7-day shield grant) — returning an XpResult. Gated in GameScreen via `rememberSaveable recorded` + `LaunchedEffect(isFinished)` so it fires exactly once and skips resumed games (deltas aren't idempotent). daily_results stays in DailyResultsService (PostGameScreen, idempotent best-score) — no double-write. Daily Sweep/Flawless bonus deferred (Android has no MedalService; server-cron + iOS only). New **XpToast.kt** ports web xp-toast.tsx/iOS XpToastView: purple-gradient pill, gold star, "+N XP", streak/daily chips, level-up line, slides in from top, 3s auto-dismiss; rendered over the victory/post-game overlays. New **WelcomeScreen.kt** ports iOS WelcomeView/web WelcomeModal (gradient accent bar, WORDOCIOUS wordmark, 3 pillars Daily Puzzles/Compete Head-to-Head/Leaderboards & Medals, username picker 3-20 validation, Get Started/Skip both set has_onboarded=true); added `has_onboarded` (default true) to Profile + gated as a cover in MainActivity when `hasOnboarded == false`. **Verified live on emulator:** played Classic daily to a loss → profile XP-to-next 375→300 (=+75: 25 base + 50 daily), win-rate 67%→50% (total_losses 1→2), streak stayed 0, daily-login recomputed to 1 (last play >1d ago) — confirms the full progression write; then played Six daily → caught the **XP toast** ("+75 XP · +50 daily") over GAME OVER. Onboarding cover is built/wired/compiles but NOT force-shown live (would require flipping the real account's has_onboarded — left untouched). **Remaining Android: #80 VS multiplayer, #85 Home Pro/Unlimited, + Google Play Billing for the Pro CTAs.***

*2026-06-04 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **BUILD 8 cut, uploaded & being swapped into App Review (replaces build 7).** After live sim co-testing, cut build 8 (CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION 8) batching everything since build 7: keyboard layout (Delete left/Enter right), live invalid-word red row + shake + "Already guessed", tile-flip reveal + winning-word-flips-before-results + victory/confetti-over-dimmed-board, full-screen game presentation (no tab bar on game/victory screens), Gauntlet active-stage glow, Recent-Matches aesthetic, completed-daily card mode-leak fix, AND the **multi-board redesign** (below). Same signing flow as 6/7: unsigned archive (`CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO`) → cloud `-exportArchive` (app-store) → **re-sign with applesignin** (cloud export strips it; `codesign -f -s "Apple Distribution: …(Q32F6GRDYG)" --entitlements <full incl. com.apple.developer.applesignin> --preserve-metadata=identifier,flags,runtime`) → repackage IPA → altool upload (Delivery UUID 07c953a1). **MULTI-BOARD REDESIGN (this session, QuadWord/OctoWord/Succession/Deliverance + Gauntlet stages; single-board untouched):** (a) **fill the screen** — web uses `1fr` rows so tiles stretch to non-square cells filling full width+height; native now matches (`TileView` optional `height`, `BoardView` fill mode w/ `tileHeight`+2px gaps, `BoardLayout` fills width AND height, 8px between boards) — fixed the big margins (esp. OctoWord, which is height-driven). (b) **per-board gray card frame** (`#E5E7EB`/white; green won/red lost) — was missing. (c) **OctoWord tap-to-zoom** — tap a board → uniform scale-from-1× morph anchored to that board's exact slot up to a centered, still-playable view + dim backdrop; tap to minimize back into the slot. matchedGeometryEffect was tried first but flew in from the top (reads source frame wrong across `.overlay`); replaced with deterministic scale+offset from the analytic slot rect (like web `getSourceTransform`). Non-uniform scale "squeezed" → switched to uniform; "started tiny" → render the enlarged board with the SAME tile layout as the mini and scale from 1× so it starts/ends at the board's real size. (d) **flip = `scaleEffect(y: cos)` squash** (= orthographic rotateX, cheaper than rotation3D). NOTE: user finds the multi-board flip **jittery on the SIMULATOR** (many tiles at once); single-board is smooth. Tried whole-row-as-one-flip (user disliked losing the stagger) → reverted to per-tile stagger. Strong hypothesis: simulator software-renderer artifact — **verify on device via build 8** before degrading the animation. **NEXT:** build 8 finishes ASC processing → swap into the review submission (remove build 7), submit, **manual release**. User TestFlight-tests build 8 on device — judge the multi-board flip smoothness for real + confetti.*

*2026-06-03 update (latest+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **Reveal/victory/nav animation polish — live sim co-testing session (all native, UNCOMMITTED, rides build 8; web edits pending push).** Iterated on game "feel" with the user watching the simulator. (1) **Tile flip reveal** — first tried a 0→180° face-swap (looked wrong / "down-to-up"); user noted web Classic doesn't flip on normal guesses but **Succession does it correctly**. Audited web: the flip is `rotateX 0°→90°→0°` (flip to edge-on and back, color applied throughout — NOT a face swap) via the `tile-flip`/`tile-flip-mini` keyframes (0.5s/150ms full, 0.3s/80ms mini). The web was INCONSISTENT — `board.tsx` (Classic/Six/Seven) flipped only the **winning** row (`isCorrect===true`) and `multi-board.tsx` (Quad/Octo/Rescue/Deliverance) only when the board **isWon**, while Succession/Gauntlet/ProperNoundle flip **every** guess. **WEB FIX (deploys on push):** relaxed `board.tsx` → `animate = isLastRow && !!evaluation` and `multi-board.tsx` `lastSubmittedRow` → every submitted row, so all modes flip every guess. **NATIVE:** added `FlipRevealTile` + `TileFlip` (orthographic squash, `perspective:0` — the 3D perspective was the "flicker"; `rotation3DEffect` axis-X, progress→0/90/0 triangle), gated to the freshly-committed last row (BoardView `seenGuessCount`, suppresses resume re-flips), mini vs full timing by `vm.isMultiBoard`. (2) **Winning word now animates before results** — `vm.isFinished` used to instantly swap the in-play board → finished/stats layout, killing the final flip. Added `revealComplete` gate: hold the in-play board until the flip plays (`revealDuration` = dur + (wordLen-1)*stagger), THEN show the victory card. (3) **Smoother victory + confetti** — victory card + confetti now spring in OVER the dimmed in-play board (`.scale(0.92)+.opacity`, spring response 0.5); the heavier finished/stats `ScrollView` is built only on "tap to continue" (`onDismiss` sets `revealComplete`), so it no longer competes with the 40-piece confetti for frames (was the choppiness cause). Confetti `Canvas`/`TimelineView` rewrite deferred — user to judge tomorrow. (4) **Bottom nav off all game screens** — the tab bar was bleeding onto the board/victory screen because games were PUSHED inside the Home tab (nav mounted behind). Root cause of the regression was the fragile `ChromeVisibility` enter/exit COUNTER drifting on unpaired appear/disappear; rewrote it to a per-screen **UUID Set** (isolated, can't be un-hidden by a stray exit), AND — the real fix — routed both GameScreen entry points (daily + unlimited) through `.fullScreenCover(item:$pendingGame)` so games present full-screen OVER the tab bar like the web's game route (added `if vm.isFinished { revealComplete=true }` onAppear guard for resumed-finished games). (5) **Gauntlet active-stage glow** — added `StageGlow` pulsing purple halo (#A855F7, shadow 3px↔7px+12px, 2.5s ease-in-out loop) on the active stage node, matching web `gauntlet-glow`; cleared=green✓, upcoming=flat grey. All reduce-motion-aware. **Cross-device mid-game persistence: TABLED** (user asked, then deferred — would need a `daily_progress` Supabase table + replay-reconstruction per mode; free in $, moderate effort + per-mode correctness risk; not standard, Wordle is per-device too). Reviewer demo account confirmed in ASC: `bterchin+appdevtester@gmail.com`. **NEXT AM:** user judges confetti smoothness (maybe Canvas rewrite); then commit native + the 2 web flip-gate edits together; build 8 batches all of the above + the prior build-8 queue (keyboard/invalid-row/shake/dup/Recent-Matches).*

Ran a full web↔native in-game UX audit (2 agents, all modes). Fixed (shared KeyboardView/BoardView/GameViewModel → applies to ALL board modes): (1) **keyboard layout** — native had ENTER-left/Delete-right; swapped to web's Delete-left/ENTER-right. (2) **live invalid row** — full-length word not in dictionary OR already guessed now turns the row red BEFORE Enter (TileView.isInvalid, red-400/50/500), matching web isInvalidWord. The toast was already at parity (white on #1a1a2e, top 90px). **PERSISTENCE CLARIFICATION:** in-progress daily time/guesses are stored PER-DEVICE on both platforms (web localStorage `wordocious-session-{mode}-daily`, native GamePersistence `wordocious-{mode}-{seed}.json` + elapsed in UserDefaults) — they do NOT sync native↔web (same as web↔web across browsers); only the COMPLETED daily result syncs via `daily_results`. Deferred (riskier SwiftUI animation; want on-device visual verification before shipping). All fixes build green; ride the next native build (8).*

*2026-06-03 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **BUILD 6 — all 3 native logins working; fixed Sign in with Apple entitlement.** TestFlight testing of build 5 on a real device: **Google ✓** (after enabling Supabase Google provider's **"Skip nonce checks"** — GoogleSignIn 7.x issues a nonce we can't echo; see [[reference_native_google_signin]]), email ✓, but **Apple failed with `ASAuthorizationError 1000`**. Also bumped CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION 6 and (earlier) made Info.plist pull `$(CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION)`/`$(MARKETING_VERSION)` + declared `ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption=false`. **BUILD-PROCESS GOTCHA (durable):** the `CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO` archive → cloud-export flow drops capability entitlements (applesignin). *2026-06-03 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **VS mode parity audit + fixes; VS now in Recent Matches.** Ran an end-to-end web↔native↔server VS audit (3 parallel mapping agents + manual verification). The socket protocol/state-machine/opponent-HUD/scoring/invites/9-modes all matched; found + fixed THREE native-only gaps: (1) **rematch was broken** — server pairs on two `offer_rematch` (no `accept_rematch` handler), but native's Accept emitted `accept_rematch` → silently dropped; Accept now re-emits `offer_rematch` (web parity); removed dead acceptRematch/VSEvent.acceptRematch. (2) **daily VS leaderboard** — native skipped `daily_results` for play_type='vs'; added `DailyResultsService.recordVs` (+vsCompositeScore, 1:1 with web recordDailyVsResult) wired into `record()`. (3) **post-match XP toast** — native discarded the XpResult; now captures + shows XpToastView on the VS result screen. THEN added **VS → Recent Matches** on BOTH platforms (was missing everywhere; `recordMatch` had no callers): client-writes design (no service-role key on the public realtime server). Server's `match_ended` now carries `opponentId` (derived from the opponent socket's presenceId `u:<id>`) + `recordMatch` (true only for player1 = single designated writer → one shared row, RLS-safe). Web vs-game + native VSMatchViewModel insert the `matches` row (player2_id set, player2_guesses null — counts only relayed). Server `tsc` also fixed (dangling QueueEntry import; runtime was fine via tsx). All build/typecheck green. NOTE: these ride **build 5** (native, not yet cut) + the server/web redeploy from this push; build 4 (in review) predates them. THEN closed the loop: added a **Recent Matches** section to the native OWN profile tab (ProfileTab) — web's own profile has it; native previously only had it on the public-profile view. Extracted a shared `RecentMatchRow` so both render identically (up to 5 rows, solo + VS, VS labelled "VS Match"). Verified VS stat writes are fully consistent web↔native — `user_stats(vs)` (identical best/avg/fastest, both store SECONDS), profiles XP/streak/level/7-day-shield (identical), `daily_results(vs)` composite, and the `matches` row. The earlier "web ms vs native seconds" worry was a MISREAD: web's recordGameResult param is `timeMs` and divides by 1000 internally, so both persist seconds — NO fix needed (dividing again would've been a 1000× bug).*

*2026-06-03 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **Auth fixes + cross-account play-limit leak (web).** Two sign-up/login bugs surfaced while creating the App Review demo account. (1) **Email signup RLS** — `auth.signUp()` then a client `profiles` insert failed "new row violates row-level security policy" because with email-confirmation ON there's no session yet (still `anon`). Fixed with a SECURITY DEFINER trigger `handle_new_user()` on `auth.users` that creates the profile from signup metadata (migration `20260603000002_auto_create_profile_on_signup.sql`, USER APPLIED — also backfilled orphaned auth users incl. `bterchin+appdevtester`); both clients now pass `{data:{username}}` and no longer insert the row. (2) **"email rate limit exceeded"** — Supabase's built-in confirmation-email cap (~3-4/hr) burned by repeated attempts; not a code bug. Unblock for the demo account: `update auth.users set email_confirmed_at = now() where email = '…' and email_confirmed_at is null;` then sign IN (no new email). Launch note: keep Google/Facebook primary or add custom SMTP for real email-signup volume. (3) **Cross-account daily-lock leak (web only)** — a brand-new account showed every mode "Play again in …" (all locked). Root cause: `play-limit-service.ts` keyed its localStorage cache by DATE ONLY (`wordocious-plays-<date>`), so a prior account's completions on the same browser leaked in (and `syncPlayLimits` only MERGED DB rows into that stale cache). Fix: scope every key per user (`wordocious-plays-<uid>-<date>`) via a persisted `activeUserId` (module var hydrated from localStorage for correct first-render), new `setActivePlayUser()` called from the home `[user]` effect + inside `syncPlayLimits`, and `cleanupOldPlayData` now purges the legacy un-scoped keys. **Native is NOT affected** — its `isLocked` reads DB daily-completions for the current user (`completions.byMode[key]`), no device-level cache. Web tsc clean. **Also (this session): export-compliance answered ("None of the algorithms" — HTTPS-only) so the uploaded build flipped Missing Compliance → Ready to Submit.***

*2026-06-03 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **Parity sweep — 4 web→native gaps closed (account deletion, avatars, daily reminder, VS-limit modal).** A full web-vs-native feature audit found most things at parity; built the 4 real gaps. (1) **Account deletion** (App Store 5.1.1(v) requirement): Settings → Delete Account → confirm alert → `AuthService.deleteAccount()` POSTs the user's access token to the EXISTING web `/api/account/delete` (the native anon client can't delete the auth user; the web route holds the service-role key and does the cascade), then signs out. (2) **Avatars**: native previously ignored `avatar_url` everywhere (initials only). New `AvatarView` (renders `avatar_url` via AsyncImage, else gradient initial) on Profile, PublicProfile, Edit Profile; `AvatarUploader` resizes to 256² JPEG → uploads to the existing `avatars` bucket at `<uid>/avatar.jpg` (lowercased uid for the RLS folder policy); Edit Profile gains a PhotosPicker "Change Photo" that writes `profiles.avatar_url` + refreshes. (3) **Daily reminder**: `NotificationService` schedules a repeating local notification (`UNUserNotificationCenter`, 6pm local — no APNs/server, the native counterpart to the web push cron) with a Settings → Notifications → Daily Reminder toggle (requests permission; reverts + alert on denial). (4) **VS-limit modal**: `DailyResultsService.hasPlayedDailyVS()` (daily_results DUEL/vs today) gates the free daily VS; when used, the lobby shows a native `VSLimitModal` (swords, "Daily VS Used", local-midnight countdown, Go Pro) mirroring `vs-limit-modal.tsx`. Build clean; verified live (Settings Delete-Account row + Daily-Reminder toggle, Edit-Profile photo picker, avatar fallback). No new migration (reuses avatars bucket). **Not gaps (web-only by nature):** PWA install prompt, cookie/consent, admin tools, service worker. **Remaining known launch blocker (unchanged):** Apple OAuth not yet configured server-side in Supabase (SIWA).*

*2026-06-03 update (latest+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **Native social share parity + a stale-install lesson.** (1) iOS Share now shows the puzzle on Facebook/X too: `ShareService` uploads the rendered PNG to the shared `share-images` bucket (path `<uid>/<ShareMode>-<date>.png`, uid LOWERCASED to satisfy the storage RLS folder policy) and includes the per-result `wordocious.com/s/…` URL in the iOS share sheet alongside the image. Messages/WhatsApp still get the file directly; Facebook/X scrape the URL's og:image (the same PNG). Verified live: share sheet shows the rich "Solved 4/6 · 0:27 · wordocious.com" preview, PNG confirmed uploaded (93.8 KB, public HTTP 200). supabase-swift 2.46 Storage `upload(_:data:options:FileOptions(contentType:upsert:))`. (2) **Lesson — stale-install false alarm:** the user reported the home header "regressed" (PRO badge, settings gear, and Daily/Unlimited toggle gone; completed dailies opening new puzzles; "old footer"). Root cause was NOT code — there are TWO DerivedData dirs for the project (`dnddssmb…` current, `dthbydbn…` from Jun 1), and the install step's `find … | head -1` is non-deterministic and had been installing YESTERDAY'S build. Confirmed via `xcrun simctl get_app_container` (installed binary mtime Jun 1 14:04 vs source/build Jun 2 13:09). Fix: install from `xcodebuild -showBuildSettings → TARGET_BUILD_DIR` (authoritative), never `find|head`. After reinstalling the correct build, ALL of it was present and correct (PRO badge, gear, toggle above the Flawless banner, and completed-daily→solved-review routing). **Going forward, always resolve the install path via TARGET_BUILD_DIR.***

*2026-06-03 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **Social share — Facebook/X now show the actual puzzle image (per-result Open Graph).** Sharing a result to Facebook/X previously showed only a generic wordocious.com link card, because those platforms refuse pre-attached image files (Web Share API `files`) and only scrape `og:image` from the shared URL — a hard platform constraint, not a fixable bug in the share button. Fix: the Share button now uploads the EXACT PNG it already generates (`generateShareImage`) to a new public `share-images` Storage bucket, and shares a per-result URL `wordocious.com/s/<uid>/<mode>-<date>?…stats` instead of the bare site URL. New route `app/s/[...key]/page.tsx` emits `openGraph`/`twitter:card` meta whose `og:image` IS that uploaded PNG (overriding the root file-convention OG image for that route), plus a human-facing landing (image + Play CTA). Messages/WhatsApp/Mail still receive the attached file directly (best quality); only the URL-scraping platforms needed this. Implementation chose upload-the-client-PNG over server re-render (`@napi-rs/canvas` not installed, Next 13.5, only Nunito-Black font on hand → re-render wouldn't be pixel-exact); uploading the real bytes guarantees the identical image. One object per (user, mode, day) with upsert (bounded growth); result stats baked into the query so distinct results are distinct URLs (no stale FB scrape cache). `shareResult` signature unchanged (uid fetched internally); falls back to the bare site URL if not signed in / upload fails. **Migration `20260603000001_create_share_images_bucket.sql` (public bucket + authed-write/public-read policies, mirrors avatars) — USER CONFIRMED APPLIED; bucket verified public via REST.** Also fixed stale mode counts (9 daily modes now): the brand OG card `og-image-renderer.tsx` "7 game modes"→"9", welcome modal "7"→"9", Pro page "all 8 game modes"→"all 9" (×2). (`/about` + `/how-to-play` left at "10" — they enumerate all 10 incl. VS Battle.) `next build` clean (`λ /s/[...key]` in manifest, 47 pages); pushed. Native share already image-only to the iOS sheet (same platform constraint applies there); a native per-result-URL share could be a future parity follow-up.*

*2026-06-02 update (latest+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **Records — "Most Games Played" & "Most Gold Medals" were missing (web AND native); root-caused + fixed.** Both record types showed blank on the all-time Records page. Diagnosed against prod via the anon REST API: `all_time_records` had **1,441 rows** against PostgREST's **1000-row cap**, because GLOBAL records (game_mode/play_type = NULL) were duplicated into the hundreds (`highest_level` 496, `most_daily_completions` 475, `most_gold_medals` 430 — each should be 1). Cause: a plain `UNIQUE(record_type, game_mode, play_type)` treats NULLs as distinct, so the app's `upsert onConflict(...)` never matched a NULL-keyed row and **inserted a fresh duplicate every game**; `checkAndUpdateRecord`'s `.maybeSingle()` also errored on the many rows so it always thought the record was new. Ordered alphabetically by `record_type`, the 996 duplicate rows pushed the two alphabetically-last types (`most_games_played`, `most_gold_medals`) entirely past row 1000, so `fetchAllTimeRecords` never returned them. **Fix:** (1) rewrote `checkAndUpdateRecord` (`lib/daily-service.ts`) to be self-healing — selects ALL rows for the key (best-first), updates the canonical row, and DELETES duplicates on every write, with no reliance on NULL `onConflict`; (2) migration `20260602000002_dedupe_all_time_records.sql` collapses existing dupes (keeping the true holder per key: MIN for time/guesses, MAX otherwise) and adds a `NULLS NOT DISTINCT` unique index so global records can never re-duplicate. Native only READS `all_time_records` (it never wrote records), so it's fixed automatically once the table is clean. Even without running the migration, the deployed write fix self-heals within one game (the next game collapses `highest_level`'s dupes, dropping the table under 1000). Web tsc clean; pushed. **ACTION: run the migration in Supabase for instant cleanup + the protective index.***

Future runs persist automatically at finish. Web tsc clean; pushed.*

*2026-06-02 update (latest+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **Gauntlet cross-device parity completed — web now READS `gauntlet_stages` too.** Finished the symmetry from the prior update: a Gauntlet played on one device shows its full results on the other, both directions. New `stats-service.fetchGauntletStages(userId, seed)` reads `matches.gauntlet_stages` (best-effort). The web `/gauntlet` page (`gauntlet-game`) now, when a daily has no local session, fetches the server breakdown and renders `GauntletResults` from it (`showPlayAgain=false`, and a new `recordOnMount=false` guard on `GauntletResults` so a cross-device revisit doesn't re-count the run in local gauntlet-stats); the leaderboard "Completed Today" card (`completed-daily-board`) gets the same fallback. Both reads are purely additive (only fire when no local session) so the on-device flow is unchanged. With native already reading + both clients writing, the rich Gauntlet results (incl. per-stage times) now render identically on web AND native regardless of where the run was played — once the `gauntlet_stages` migration is applied and a gauntlet is played after. Web tsc clean; committed + pushed.*

*2026-06-02 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **Gauntlet per-stage breakdown persisted server-side (cross-device parity).** The Gauntlet results' per-stage data (name, status, guesses, **per-stage timeMs**, and each stage's final `boardsSnapshot`) previously lived ONLY in the playing device's local storage; the server (`matches`) only had the run total — so a cross-device revisit couldn't reproduce it (per-stage time can't be split out of one total). Fix: new column **`matches.gauntlet_stages` (jsonb)** = `{ stages, stageResults }`, written by both clients as a SEPARATE best-effort update after the match insert (`GameResultsService.recordGauntletStages` / web `recordGauntletStages`) — best-effort so a not-yet-applied column never breaks recording (social_links lesson). The JSON decodes 1:1 on both platforms (shapes verified: `GauntletStageConfig/Result`, `BoardState`, `GuessResult`, `GameStatus` all identical). **Native reads** it (`MatchStatsService.gauntletStages(seed:)`) as a fallback in `SolvedPuzzleView` + leaderboard `CompletedDailyCard` when there's no local session, rebuilding a `GauntletProgress` so the rich stage breakdown shows cross-device. **Migration `20260602000001_add_matches_gauntlet_stages.sql` must be run in the Supabase SQL editor** (`alter table public.matches add column if not exists gauntlet_stages jsonb;`) for writes to persist — until then it no-ops harmlessly and cross-device gauntlet detail stays off. Web tsc clean; native builds; committed + pushed. **Remaining symmetric piece (offered, not yet done):** a server-read fallback on the WEB (so a native-played gauntlet shows the rich screen on web) — the web still reads its own localStorage on revisit, which covers the device-where-played (the common case).*

(NATIVE `SolvedPuzzleView` gauntlet branch) Rebuilt to mirror the web `GauntletResults`: amber trophy + "GAUNTLET CLEARED!" gradient title (XCircle + "GAUNTLET FAILED" on loss), three boxed stat cards (Stages X/5 · Guesses · Time), `DailyRankBadge`, the shared `ScoreBreakdownView` for GAUNTLET (1000 + time + completion → matches the web's 2,475), a "STAGE BREAKDOWN" section (expandable per-stage mini-boards via `GauntletCompletedView(showSummary:false, showStageHeader:true)`), and bottom Share/Home buttons (corner Home hidden on this screen to avoid a duplicate). **Device-local caveat (same on web):** the rich stage-by-stage view needs the local saved `GameState.gauntlet` (per-stage `boardsSnapshot`) — exactly like the web reads its own `localStorage`. A gauntlet played on another device falls back to the generic board-grid reconstruction (the synced `matches` row has the 21 flat solutions but no per-stage breakdown/times). So on the sim a web-played gauntlet shows the fallback; playing the gauntlet natively shows the rich screen. Web tsc clean; native builds; committed + pushed.*

*2026-06-02 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++++++): **FAQ / How-to-Play scoring rewritten for accuracy + hints (web AND native, identical).** The FAQ's "How are scores calculated?" was wrong — it described a general guess bonus and a 1,687-pt Classic example, but the engine (`DailyScoring.swift` ↔ `lib/daily-service.ts`) only awards a guess bonus to the hint modes (Six/Seven/ProperNoundle); a real Classic score is base 1,000 + speed (timeCap − seconds) + completion (≤200, scaled by boards solved), e.g. a 27s solve = 1,000 + 273 + 200 = 1,473 (matches the in-app Score Breakdown). Rewrote the FAQ with identical copy in `apps/web/components/modals/help-modal.tsx` and `apps/ios/.../HelpView.swift`, and fixed the web `/how-to-play` Scoring System section the same way: corrected the components + example, and added **hints** — each revealed hint subtracts 120 (ProperNoundle) / 150 (Six, Seven), floored at zero, other modes unaffected. (top-3 daily podium + streak/perfect milestones), and enriched streak (local-midnight reset), Streak-Shield (auto on first miss, header count), and PRO (ad-free + unlimited replays + Unlimited mode + Pro Insights + VS invites/rematches) answers. Native FAQ verified live; web tsc clean. Committed + pushed (Vercel auto-deploys the web copy).*
