*2026-06-02 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++++): **WEB fix — completed daily boards intermittently blew up & clipped on the leaderboard.** On wordocious.com's COMPLETED TODAY card (`completed-daily-board.tsx` `CompletedMiniBoard`), the first row of mini boards sometimes rendered oversized and clipped on the right while the rest stayed small, and a re-render (tab blur→focus) could flip a correct layout into the broken one. Root cause: CSS-grid blowout — grid items default to `min-width:auto` and the tiles use `aspectRatio:1`, so on some layout passes a tile sized from its stretched row height and overflowed its column, dragging the board past its cell. Fix: `min-w-0`/`min-h-0` at every grid level + `minmax(0,1fr)` columns and `minmax(0,auto)` rows so width stays authoritative and tiles shrink to their cell — deterministic now. Also covers Gauntlet's completed stage boards (reuse `CompletedMiniBoard`); ProperNoundle's mini board uses explicit pixel widths and was unaffected. Web typecheck clean; pushed to main (Vercel auto-deploy). (NOTE: this WEB bug is distinct from the native completed-board work below — the user was viewing wordocious.com.)*

*2026-06-02 update (latest+++++++++++++++++++++++): **iOS Succession (and all-mode) completed-board rendering fixed + audited.** The solved/completed views (`SolvedPuzzleView` + leaderboard `CompletedDailyCard`) reconstructed each board by replaying ONE shared flat guess list against every solution. Correct for Quordle/Octordle (all boards receive the same guesses simultaneously) but WRONG for **Succession** (`SEQUENCE`), which is played one board at a time — so a late-solved board replayed the whole list and ballooned to ~14 ragged, mismatched rows. Fix: (a) **prefer the local saved per-board `GameState`** (`GamePersistence.load(seed,mode)`), which is correct for every mode and present whenever this device played the daily; (b) when absent (cross-device, e.g. the daily was played on the web), reconstruct **mode-aware** via a new `CompletedBoardReconstruct` — SEQUENCE splits the flat list sequentially (advance to the next board when a guess matches the current solution), all other multi modes keep shared-guess semantics. Both views now render through one shared `CompletedMiniBoardView`, padded to the largest board's capacity for a uniform grid, framed green/red with a ✓ badge (single board unframed, like the web). **Audited every mode's completed screen vs wordocious.com and verified live in the sim**: Classic (single, unframed ✓), QuadWord (2×2 shared), OctoWord (4×2 shared ✓), Succession (2×2 sequential — each board its own 4/5/6/7-guess run ending on its solution ✓); Deliverance/Six/Seven flow through the same shared/single paths (not completable today to screenshot); ProperNoundle keeps its own restore path (`ProperNoundleView`). **Known follow-up:** Gauntlet's completed view uses the generic board grid; the web has a dedicated stage-by-stage `GauntletCompletedCard` (needs a completed Gauntlet daily to build+verify).*

*2026-06-02 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++++): **iOS daily/completed/unlimited + Word-of-the-Day fixes (all verified live in the sim).** Five issues the user hit on the running build. (1) **Word of the Day was stale** — Home showed yesterday's word (Scram) while web showed today's (Drift). The day-index math (`floor(ms/86400000)`) and the bundled `solutions.json` are byte-identical to the web, so the word was correct — the bug was caching: `WordOfTheDayView` used `.task { if info == nil }`, which fetches once and never refreshes; the Home tab stays alive in the TabView, so across the UTC-day rollover it kept the prior word. Now re-fetches keyed on the UTC day index (+ scenePhase so foregrounding re-checks). (2) **Completed daily boards didn't fit one screen** — OctoWord clipped on the leaderboard and the solved views were oversized/scrolly. Ported the web `completed-daily-board.tsx` sizing into a shared `CompletedBoardLayout`: a width-capped grid (4 cols + min(320pt) for >4 boards, 2 cols + 240pt for 2-4, 200pt single) with a solved-for responsive tile size so EVERY board fits with no scroll/clip. Applied to `CompletedDailyCard` (leaderboard, now also with filler rows + per-board green/red frame + ✓ badge) and `SolvedPuzzleView`. (3) **Tapping a completed daily replayed it instead of showing the review** — `isLocked` only gated free users, so Pro users fell through to a `NavigationLink` that started a fresh game on the same daily seed. Now a finished daily (Daily mode) routes to the solved-puzzle review for everyone (`solvedMode` → `SolvedPuzzleView`/`ProperNoundleView`); free users keep the upsell modal (which already links to View Solved). (4) **Games weren't full-screen** — the bottom tab nav (a `safeAreaInset` on the TabView) bled onto pushed game/solved screens, stealing the height that crushed boards tiny and cut off the keyboard. Added a shared `ChromeVisibility` + `.hidesBottomNav()` on `GameScreen`/`SolvedPuzzleView`/`ProperNoundleView` so they're full-screen like the web. (5) **OctoWord boards were tiny** — `BoardLayout` used 2 columns for 8 boards (→ 4 rows → fit-to-height crush); the web uses **4 columns** (`grid-cols-4`) for octordle. Native now matches (4 cols >4 boards, 2 for 2-4, 1 single). (6) **Unlimited didn't persist** — it minted a new timestamp seed every tap, so an in-progress puzzle never resumed; now the Unlimited seed resolves at tap time (resume the saved non-daily puzzle while it's still PLAYING, else mint+remember a fresh seed), so an Unlimited puzzle persists until finished and the completed-review path only fires in Daily mode — web parity. **Verified live**: Home shows 'Drift'; the OctoWord COMPLETED TODAY leaderboard card and the tap-through solved review both render all 8 boards in a clean 4×2 grid, full-screen, fully visible, with the score breakdown — no replay, no nav bar.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++++++++++++++++++): **iOS parity batch — 6 functional items + 3 minor polishes (all built clean, pushed to main).** Closed the remaining web-parity gaps found in the last audit. (1) **Unlimited mode records stats** — non-daily (Unlimited) plays now write `user_stats` + a `matches` row (just not `daily_results`), so the Unlimited hero's "All stats count" is true; one-line guard change in `GameViewModel.recordResultIfNeeded` (only the daily-results write stays daily-gated, as before). (2) **Pro Insights depth** — extended `MatchStatsService.proInsights` with parallel sub-fetchers porting the rest of `pro-insights-card.tsx`: `fetchWordInsights` (nemesis word, lucky word, avg guesses, first-try rate), `fetchModeWinStreak` (current/best streak), `peakHourLabel`, `fetchHeadToHeadRecord` (VS W/L/win-rate). `ProInsightsCard` now shows new stat cells (Win Streak, Avg Guesses, First Try Rate, Peak Hour, Lucky Word) + a Nemesis row (≥2 losses) + a VS Record row. (3) **Post-game Daily Rank badge** — ported `daily-rank-badge.tsx` to a `DailyRankBadge` (Top X% · #rank of total, gold when ≥75th pct) shown on the finished `GameScreen` and `SolvedPuzzleView` for daily plays, fetched via `LeaderboardService.userRank`. (4) **Medals on Profile** — new `MedalsService` reads the `medals` table; Profile shows a gold/silver/bronze tally (from `profiles.*_medals`) + a recent-medals list (crown/medal/flame/star icons per `medal_type`). (5) **Edit Profile** — `EditProfileView` sheet (Edit-profile button in the Profile header) edits username (validated) + social links (twitter/instagram/tiktok/threads/discord/website), saved to `profiles`; links render as tappable chips. **`social_links` is fetched via a SEPARATE optional query (`ProfileExtras.socialLinks`) and is NOT in the main profile `select`** — same resilience lesson as the ProperNoundle constraint: a column missing from prod must never break profile loading. Avatar upload deferred. (6) **Onboarding** — `WelcomeView` first-run modal (ports `welcome-modal.tsx`): wordmark + 3 pillars + username field, shown via `fullScreenCover` when `profiles.has_onboarded == false`; Get Started / Skip both set `has_onboarded=true`. **Minors:** (a) Daily/Unlimited choice already persists via `@AppStorage("pref-play-mode")` (String enum) — confirmed, no change needed. (b) **ProperNoundle VS board now shows the Clue/Vowel/Consonant hint row** — extracted a shared `NoundleHints` view reused by the solo screen + `ProperNoundleVSBoard`. (c) **Gauntlet VS stage relay** — `GameViewModel` fires a new `onStageCompleted(stageIndex)` when a stage clears in a VS run; `VSMatchViewModel` emits `stage_completed` and handles `opponent_stage_completed` (the `VSMatchService` plumbing already existed), tracking `opponent.stagesCleared`; the opponent strip now shows the opponent's current Gauntlet stage. **Caveats:** Pro Insights / medals / rank badge depend on real recorded data and are not screenshot-verified end-to-end; VS stage relay needs the (down) socket server to verify live — code-complete, same caveat as all native VS. All changes build clean and are pushed to `main` across 4 commits.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest++++++++++++++++++++): **iOS: fixed a VS join crash (all modes).** Tapping a VS mode (reported on ProperNoundle, but mode-agnostic) hard-crashed in `VSMatchService.decode`: `JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject:)` *raises an NSException* — not a Swift error, so `try?` cannot catch it — when the socket payload's first element isn't a valid top-level JSON object (a String/Number/NSNull error payload, an ack, etc.), which aborts the app (SIGABRT, the exact crash in the user's report). Added a `JSONSerialization.isValidJSONObject(first)` guard before serializing so an unexpected/malformed payload is skipped instead of crashing. **Verified in the sim**: ProperNoundle VS now reaches the "Pairing… / Position in queue" screen (previously crashed on tap). This was a pre-existing latent bug in the VS socket layer, surfaced now that all modes are tappable in the VS lobby; the fix protects every VS mode.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++++++++++++++++): **iOS: VS for Gauntlet + ProperNoundle (both now populate under VS Battle).** Matches the web, which has dedicated `VsGauntlet` + `VsProperNoundle`. Added `.gauntlet` and `.propernoundle` to the VS lobby `modes` (quick-match + create-private-match grids) and to the Home Unlimited VS-swords overlay. **Gauntlet VS** reuses the shared board path (`GameViewModel(.gauntlet, isVersus:true)` — the core engine handles gauntlet); `VSGameView` now renders the stage-clear **Continue/Finish Gauntlet** button in a match (was keyboard-only, so a cleared stage couldn't advance). **ProperNoundle VS** is bespoke (own engine): `ProperNoundleVM` gained a seed-based init + VS hooks (`onGuessCommitted`/`onCompleted`, `isVersus` skips solo recording); `VSMatchViewModel` drives a `ProperNoundleVM` (new `@Published var proper`) for `.propernoundle` instead of a board `GameViewModel`; `VSGameView` renders a new `ProperNoundleVSBoard` (reuses `NoundleBoard`/`NoundleKeyboard`). Added `ProperNoundle.puzzle(forSeed:)` — a stable FNV-1a hash → list index so both players get the same puzzle (Swift's String.hashValue is per-launch randomized, so a fixed hash was required). **Verified in the sim**: both appear in the VS Battle lobby (quick + private). Live match play still needs the VS socket server (down per VS_SETUP), so this is code-complete but not live-verified — same caveat as all native VS. Builds clean.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest++++++++++++++++++): **iOS Home: Daily Challenge hero fills the Daily slot (no layout shift between Daily/Unlimited).** The Daily mode had no banner where Unlimited shows its `UnlimitedHero`, so toggling shifted the Word-of-the-Day + game-cards grid. Added `DailyChallengeHero` ("★ Daily Challenge ★ / N puzzles · Leaderboards & medals / Resets in HH:MM:SS", violet→indigo gradient, ports the web Daily hero), sized to match `UnlimitedHero` and the Sweep/Flawless `banner` (same py + 3 lines). The Home hero slot is now ALWAYS filled: Unlimited → `UnlimitedHero`; all dailies done → Sweep/Flawless `banner`; otherwise → `DailyChallengeHero`. So switching Daily⇄Unlimited (or completing all dailies) never shifts the cards. The Profile Today's-Dailies card already swaps to Flawless Victory / Daily Sweep when all 9 are complete (unchanged). Verified live in the sim (Daily Challenge banner + countdown shows; same slot/size as the Unlimited hero). Builds clean.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++++++++++++++): **iOS Home Pro parity — Daily/Unlimited toggle + Unlimited hero, web-matching footer, PRO-badge clip fix.** (1) **Daily | Unlimited toggle** (`PlayModeToggle`, Pro-only, ports play-mode-toggle.tsx) + **`UnlimitedHero`** ("∞ Unlimited Play ∞ / Infinite puzzles · All stats count / Tap ⚔ on any game for VS"). In Unlimited mode each standard card launches a **fresh non-daily game** (seed `unlimited-<mode>-<ts>`, no daily gate) and shows a **VS swords overlay** (Pro+Unlimited, excludes VS/Gauntlet/ProperNoundle) that pushes the VS lobby via `navigationDestination` (system back button — first tried a fullScreenCover but it trapped the user with no back). Free users never see the toggle (forced to Daily). (2) **PRO badge was clipped** to an orange sliver next to the wordmark — the header was overflowing; the wordmark now uses `minimumScaleFactor(0.6)` instead of fixed-size so "WORDOCIOUS PRO" + the 4 controls fit one row. (3) **Footer now matches the web `BottomNav`**: replaced the system `UITabBar` with a custom `BottomNav` — outline icon + muted label when inactive, **filled icon + purple label + a 4px dot** when active. The system tab bar is hidden (`.toolbar(.hidden, for: .tabBar)`) and the custom nav is added via `safeAreaInset(.bottom)` with `background(…, ignoresSafeAreaEdges: .bottom)` so it fills the home-indicator zone; `TabView(selection:)` is kept so each tab preserves its state, and the per-tab ad banner still insets above the nav. **Verified live in the sim**: PRO badge fully visible, toggle switches Daily⇄Unlimited, hero + per-card swords render, swords→VS lobby, Unlimited card → fresh game (no ad for Pro), footer active/inactive states. Minor: `@AppStorage` play-mode persistence didn't survive a reinstall in testing (in-session works); not pursued.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest++++++++++++++++): **iOS: shared web-style header across all 4 tabs.** Ported the web `AppHeader` to a shared `AppHeaderView` now used on Home, Leaderboard, Profile, and Records (previously the header was Home-only, via a nav-bar toolbar, and Profile had a separate floating gear). It renders the **WORDOCIOUS gradient wordmark (no background/bubble)** + PRO badge when active, then **help** and **settings** circle buttons, then the **daily-streak** (🔥) and **streak-shield** (🛡) pills. Changes: (1) settings moved into the header — removed the floating Profile toolbar gear; (2) streak/shield pills show the real `profiles.daily_login_streak` / `streak_shields` and are now **tappable** → a current/best + explanation popover (ports the web `StatPopover`; uses `presentationCompactAdaptation(.popover)` guarded for iOS 16.4+); (3) the streak pill was wrapping its 2-digit count into a squished circle — added `fixedSize`/`lineLimit(1)` so it's the **same capsule shape/size as the shield**, and sized the wordmark to 20 (web `text-xl`) so all controls fit one row. Each tab root hides the system nav bar (`.toolbar(.hidden, for: .navigationBar)`); pushed detail views keep their own bars. **Verified live in the sim** across all four tabs incl. the working streak popover (Current 35 / Best 35). Note: streak + shields were already wired to real data on Home — this makes them consistent app-wide + interactive. Builds clean.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++++++++++++): **iOS Profile "All"-view Pro Stats section added (completes Pro-stats web parity).** The remaining gap from the previous update: the web's All-view **Pro Stats** (`components/profile/pro-stats.tsx`) — two aggregate bar charts, **Win Rate by Mode** (#facc15 yellow, 0–100%) and **Avg Solve Time by Mode** (#a78bfa purple) — wasn't on native. Added `ProStatsCard` (Swift Charts), shown on the All view (`mode == nil`): free users get the locked "Pro Feature" teaser + gold Upgrade-to-Pro button (→ ProView); Pro users get the two charts, built from `user_stats` (combined solo+vs per mode, the native convention; games-weighted avg time; short mode labels Classic/Quad/Octo/Succ/Deliv/Six/Seven/Gaunt/Proper). Per-mode view still shows `ProInsightsCard`. **Verified live in the sim** — free shows the locked teaser; Simulate Pro shows both bar charts; reverted Simulate Pro after (writes `profiles.is_pro`). So the Profile Pro tiering now matches the web on BOTH the per-mode (Pro Insights) and All (Pro Stats) views. Builds clean.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest++++++++++++++): **iOS Profile settings gear pinned to a solid header.** The gear was a `topBarTrailing` toolbar item but the nav bar had no background (transparent), so the scrolling Profile content slid under the floating icon — it read as the button "hovering through the scroll." Added `.toolbarBackground(Theme.background, for: .navigationBar)` + `.toolbarBackground(.visible, …)` so the gear sits on a pinned, solid (theme-aware) header bar and content scrolls beneath it. Verified in the sim. Builds clean.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++++++++++): **iOS Profile: Pro Insights now shows a locked "Upgrade to Pro" teaser to free users (web parity).** Audit of how the web gates Pro stats (`components/profile/pro-insights-card.tsx`): when NOT Pro it renders a **locked overlay** (frosted card + `Lock` #c4b5fd + "Deep Insights" + a gold `linear-gradient(#f59e0b,#d97706)` Crown "Upgrade to Pro" → /pro), and when Pro it shows the real per-mode insights. Native was *hiding* the per-mode `ProInsightsCard` entirely for free users (`if let mode, isProActive`). Fixed: the card now always renders for a selected mode and self-gates — free users get the locked teaser (frosted placeholder + lock + "Deep Insights" + gold "Upgrade to Pro" button that presents `ProView`), Pro users get the real stats (Fastest Win, Fewest Guesses, Perfect Games, Consistency, trend); the proInsights query only runs when Pro. **Verified live in the sim**: Classic mode selected → free shows the locked teaser; "Simulate Pro" on → real per-game insights (8s fastest, 3 fewest, trend). Reverted Simulate Pro after (it writes `profiles.is_pro`). Builds clean. Remaining web-parity gap (minor, not requested): the web's **"All"-view "Pro Stats"** section (two aggregate bar charts — Win Rate by Mode, Avg Solve Time by Mode, also locked for free) isn't on native's All view yet — native's All view shows the free charts only. Per-mode Pro Insights (the reported behavior) is now matched.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest++++++++++++): **iOS: ProperNoundle now selectable in the Leaderboard + Records mode pickers.** ProperNoundle was the only daily mode missing from the leaderboard's top mode selector (and the Records by-mode pickers). Root cause (same family as the earlier Profile Today's-Dailies grid miss): those pickers filtered/matched on `HomeMode.mode` — a `GameMode` — which is `nil` for ProperNoundle (it runs its own engine, so its HomeMode has `dbKey:"PROPERNOUNDLE"` but `mode:nil`). Fix: the leaderboard picker, the shared `HModePicker` (All-Time + Daily records), and the All-Time/Daily title-accent lookups now key off `dbKey` instead — pickers include every dbKey-bearing mode (9, VS excluded) and map the chosen `dbKey` to a `GameMode` via `GameMode(rawValue:)` (`"PROPERNOUNDLE"` == `GameMode.propernoundle`, rawValue confirmed in packages/core). The leaderboard fetch already used `mode.rawValue`, so selecting ProperNoundle queries `daily_results.game_mode = 'PROPERNOUNDLE'` correctly. Verified in the sim: ProperNoundle appears in the picker and loads its daily leaderboard (currently "No results yet" — expected, since ProperNoundle daily recording only started working after today's constraint fix). Builds clean.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++++++++): **iOS: ad banner no longer blocks bottom content on Profile / Leaderboard / Records.** The bottom banner was applied via `.adBanner()` *outside* each tab's `NavigationStack` (in `RootTabView`), so the inner `ScrollView` never inset for the 50pt banner and the last items — notably Profile's **Sign out** button — sat underneath it, unreachable. Moved `.adBanner()` *inside* each of those three `NavigationStack` roots (matching the earlier Home fix), so the ScrollView insets correctly and bottom content scrolls fully above the banner; this also keeps the banner off pushed detail views (e.g. a solved board opened from those tabs). Verified in the sim (Sign out now reachable above the banner). Builds clean.*

*2026-06-01 update (latest++++++++++): **Accessibility + sound settings made functional (both platforms).** Audit found that of the six Settings toggles, only **web sound** actually did anything — the other five were dead (saved but never read), the same pattern as themes. Fixes: **Colorblind mode** — now swaps tiles/keyboard to the high-contrast palette (orange=correct #f5793a, blue=present #85c0f9) for red-green color blindness, on native (`Theme.correct/present/keyCorrect/keyPresent` read `ThemeManager.colorblind`) and web (`[data-colorblind]` overrides the hardcoded green/yellow tile/key classes 500+600 — the old `--correct/--present` vars were never referenced, so the prior approach was a no-op). **Verified live in the sim** (solved board tiles render orange/blue). **Reduced motion** — now actually cuts animations: native adds `Theme.animation()` (nil when reduced) gating the app's `.animation`/`withAnimation` sites; web sets `[data-reduced-motion]` driving a global CSS kill-switch (the old `--transition-duration` var was unused). **Native sound effects** — native had **no audio at all** (haptics only); added `SoundManager` that synthesizes the web `lib/sounds.ts` tones via AVAudioEngine (key tap + win/loss/invalid jingles, `.ambient` session so it respects the mute switch), gated by `pref-sound`, wired into KeyboardView / GameScreen win-loss / GameViewModel invalid / ProperNoundle. Web sound already worked (Web Audio). `ThemeManager` now owns colorblind + reducedMotion (published + persisted); Settings toggles drive it. Native builds clean, web tsc clean. Notes: native colorblind/reduced-motion apply on the next screen render (not forced root rebuild, so toggling doesn't dismiss Settings); the colorblind hex palette matches across platforms. (Sound/reduced-motion aren't screenshot-verifiable; verified by code + build. The colorblind toggle was left ON in the test sim — a harmless local device preference.)*

*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++++++): **Themes made real on both platforms (Default / Dark / Ocean / Forest).** Themes were half-built everywhere: native saved `pref-theme` but `Theme` was static colors (no-op picker); web's `SettingsDialog` (theme + sound + colorblind + reduced-motion) was **never rendered anywhere** (no settings button at all — `ThemeProvider` mounted but unreachable); and Ocean/Forest only overrode `--correct/--present/--absent`, which **aren't referenced anywhere**, so even on web they were no-ops (only Dark did a real repaint). Fixes — owner chose "ship it properly (both)": (1) **Native** — new `ThemeManager` (ObservableObject) + a `Palette` per theme; `Theme`'s surface/text/border tokens now read the active palette and the app root rebuilds on change (`preferredColorScheme` + `.id`) so every screen recolors live (tiles/keyboard/brand stay constant, matching web Dark). Settings picker writes through `ThemeManager`. **Verified live in the sim** — Dark = full dark-navy repaint, Ocean = blue tint; changing theme returns you to Home (the root rebuild that repaints also resets nav — acceptable for a set-once choice). (2) **Web** — added a gear button to the shared `AppHeader` (next to Help) that opens the orphaned `SettingsDialog`; theming applies via the already-mounted `ThemeProvider`/`[data-theme]`. (3) **Ocean/Forest given full real palettes** on both — native `ThemeManager` + matching `[data-theme="ocean"|"forest"]` `--color-*` overrides in globals.css (blue/teal + green/earth light tints). Ocean/Forest hexes are freshly designed (web never had real ones) — tweakable. tsc clean; native builds clean. Known follow-up: the native `.id`-rebuild kicks you back to Home on theme change (a full no-refactor recolor; in-place would need every view to observe the theme).*

*2026-06-01 update (latest++++++++): **Native iOS app merged to `main` + ProperNoundle Today's-Dailies grid fix.** (1) **Merged the full native SwiftUI iOS app to `main`** via PR #38 (`Native SwiftUI iOS app (Wordocious)`, merge commit `e776f81`) — 54 commits, all of `apps/ios`. It had been living on the feature branch `claude/eloquent-hodgkin-383095`; it's now on `main` alongside the web app. The merge preserved the two web-only fixes already on `main` (single-board completion-bonus row + the ProperNoundle constraint migration) since the branch never touched those files. Note: merging `apps/ios` does NOT affect the Vercel web deploy (nothing under `apps/web` changed in the PR). (2) **Fixed a native-only UI bug:** ProperNoundle wasn't rendering in the Profile "Today's Dailies" grid (you'd see "/9" with only 8 tiles). The grid filtered modes on `dbKey != nil && mode != nil`, but ProperNoundle has a `dbKey` and no `GameMode` enum (its own engine), so it was dropped. Added a dedicated `dailyTiles` list (`dbKey != nil`, 9 modes, VS excluded) for the grid — web parity 5+4 — and taught `dailyBadge` to route ProperNoundle to `ProperNoundleView` (which restores the solved daily when already played); the GameMode-backed mode-picker list is unchanged. This is a UI-list fix independent of the constraint fix below — it does not "heal" over time. Verified live in the sim (crown ProperNoundle tile now in row 2). Builds clean; committed `c236146`.*
*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++++): **ProperNoundle daily-recording bug fixed (prod DB constraint).** Symptom: finishing the daily ProperNoundle greyed/locked the Home card (freemium `play_limits` write succeeded) but showed no W/L badge and wasn't counted in the Profile "Today's Dailies" — on web AND native (same tables). Root cause: `recordGameResult` writes `game_mode='PROPERNOUNDLE'` into `user_stats` + `daily_results`, but the **production** `game_mode` CHECK constraints predated the mode (only DUEL/QUORDLE/OCTORDLE/SEQUENCE/RESCUE/GAUNTLET) — the repo constraint migrations (20260414000001 / 20260521000001) had never been applied to prod. supabase-js returns `{error}` (doesn't throw) on a constraint violation and the result wasn't inspected → silent no-row, while the lock (separate unconstrained `play_limits` table) still wrote. Fix: (1) new idempotent migration `20260601000001_reapply_game_mode_constraints.sql` re-states every constraint (daily_seeds/daily_results/matches/user_stats) with the full mode list incl. PROPERNOUNDLE/DUEL_6/DUEL_7; (2) **owner applied it to prod via the Supabase SQL editor** (project `eniiqqsxpmuyrspvepiw`) — confirmed success; (3) hardened `recordDailyResult` to inspect the insert/update `.error` so a rejected daily write can never fail silently again. Records correctly from the next fresh play onward (today's already-played row was rejected pre-fix and isn't backfilled). Also clears the same latent issue for Six/Seven. (CLI link/push not used — entering the Supabase access token / DB password is a hard no, so the owner ran the SQL in-dashboard.)*

*2026-06-01 update (latest++++++): **iOS game-screen layout + finished-screen parity fixes (verified live in the sim).** (1) **Boards now fit the screen** — `BoardLayout` sizes tiles to the smaller of the width and (in-play) height budget via a GeometryReader instead of fixed per-mode sizes in a ScrollView, so Deliverance/QuadWord/OctoWord (and wide Six/Seven single boards) never get clipped by the keyboard; post-game keeps width-fit + scroll. Applied to `GameScreen` + `VSGameView`. (2) **Ad banner no longer covers the keyboard or tab bar** — the `.adBanner()` inset was leaking onto pushed game screens (and earlier was on the TabView, covering the footer); moved inside the Home `NavigationStack` root so it shows on the mode grid only, off all pushed game screens. (3) **Finished/solved screens now match wordocious.com** — added a shared `SolvedBoardFrame` (green frame #4ade80 / bg #f0fdf4 + green ✓ badge on each solved board; red frame on unsolved-at-loss) applied in `BoardView` (live, the moment a board is won) and `SolvedPuzzleView`, plus a shared `FinishedStatsHeader` (amber-trophy boards-solved · guesses · blue-clock time + green/red summary + Home/Share) used by both the live post-game `GameScreen` and the reconstructed solved view; solved boards are now equal-height with empty white filler rows (was trimming). Verified live in the sim across single-board (Classic — no frame, no trophy), 4-board (QuadWord loss — 3 green + 1 red frame, red "3/4 solved"), and 8-board (OctoWord — 8 green frames + ✓, "All 8 solved") solved views, matching the user-supplied web reference.*
*2026-06-01 update (latest+++++): **Full aesthetic polish pass (web-parity).** Ran a multi-agent audit workflow (11 screens audited in parallel, each finding adversarially verified) → 77 verified pixel/copy/color discrepancies vs wordocious.com. Implemented ~75 across every screen, building + committing per area: shared Theme tokens (gray-300 empty border, borderLight/divider, darker 600-weight keyboard palette); Home (split Sweep/Flawless banners w/ icons+gradient text, flush gradient accent bar, W/L badge, exact card/grid sizing); Game (persistent corner Home button, keyboard palette, post-game status line, underlined actions, rounded toast, black tile weight); Pro (floating BEST VALUE badge, crown/title sizes, bold weights); Auth (purple 3D submit gradient, styled error box, card glow/radius); Help; Settings (two sections) + Info (section cards); ProperNoundle (flat red title, per-category pill, per-button hint colors, clock/delete icons, ENTER-right); VS (per-mode 3-stop title gradients, result headline hexes, sizes); Leaderboard (gradient header + live countdown, compact yesterday rows); Records (inner gold/surface cards, custom Solo|VS toggle); Profile (amber PRO badge, banner, daily badges). Then completed the two largest deferred items too: the **Profile mode-picker rebuild** (leading "All" chip + vertical chips with games-count badges, nil-selection = global "All" dashboard) and the **Records daily-leaderboard card** (accent bar + mode header + inline Solo|VS toggle + player-count/rank row). So all ~77 audit findings are now implemented. Also added the remaining Profile dashboard extras — **Top Words** (most-guessed words + win%, from `matches.player1_guesses`) and **Pro Insights** (per-mode personal bests, perfect games, consistency score, improvement trend; Pro-gated) — so the Profile data-viz is complete. All builds clean; signed-out Auth polish visually verified in the simulator (purple btn-3d gradient, #c4b5fd card border + glow). **Visual sweep done on a real signed-in account** (native Google OAuth now wired live — owner added the `com.wordocious.app://auth-callback` redirect to Supabase, so native Google sign-in is verified end-to-end): confirmed Home (accent bars + W/L badges), Profile (rebuilt mode picker w/ All chip + games counts, all charts, Top Words, calendar), Records Daily (card restructure) + All-Time (Hall of Fame + by-mode inner cards), Game (corner Home button, light tile borders), and Auth (purple btn-3d). The sweep caught + fixed a real bug: `session.user.id.uuidString` is uppercase but Postgres returns uuids lowercase, so client-side `winner_id`/`player1_id` string compares failed → Top Words showed 0% win and the calendar rendered no green days; fixed by lowercasing the uid in `MatchStatsService`. **Freemium daily-play gating added + verified live** (ports page.tsx + mode-limit-modal.tsx): free users see completed dailies greyed/locked on Home; tapping shows the "Played Today" modal (countdown + Upgrade to Pro + View Solved Puzzle); Pro users replay unlimited. Added a TEST-ONLY "Simulate Pro"/"Disable Pro" toggle in the Profile header (flips `profiles.is_pro`, mirrors the web dev toggle) — **remove before launch.** Verified the full free↔Pro cycle on a real account in the sim. **Four more parity items (all verified live):** (1) **Leaderboard completed-board dropdown** — `CompletedDailyCard`, a collapsible "Completed/Attempted Today" card that expands to the user's solved board (from the matches row), ports completed-daily-board.tsx. (2) **Today's Dailies badges are tappable** — played → solved board, not-played → play the daily. (3) **Profile equal sizing** — summary cells + mode-picker chips now reserve their optional lines so all are uniform height. (4) **Achievements** — `AchievementService` (all 70 defs 1:1 with achievement-service.ts) + a collapsible "ACHIEVEMENTS X/70" grid in Profile (unlocked purple+✓, locked faded+?), reading the shared `achievements` table. Collapsible headers made fully tappable via contentShape.
**checkAchievements ported to native** — `AchievementService.checkAchievements` is a 1:1 port of the web's ~580-line unlock logic (all 70 achievements), wired into every completion path (solo `GameViewModel`, `VSMatchViewModel`, `ProperNoundleVM`), inserting newly-unlocked keys into the shared `achievements` table. ProperNoundle now also writes a `recordSoloMatch` row (with `hints_used`) so the `pure_proper` hintless ladder + charts work. So native plays now unlock achievements the same as web. Combined the web's per-column profile reads into single fetches; uses local `today` to match native `daily_results.day`. Builds clean; queries are `try?`-guarded (graceful, no crash on schema drift).
**"View Solved Puzzle" now reconstructs from the server `matches` row** (`MatchStatsService.solvedDaily` + read-only `SolvedPuzzleView`) so it works cross-device / on a fresh install, not just from the local snapshot. Verified live: a locked Classic → View Solved Puzzle rebuilt the exact GHOST/DRINK/PLACE/MOLDY/MOULD board with the 1,469 breakdown (Win 1000 + Time 269 + Completion 200), re-confirming the completion-bonus scoring fix on native. (Verification: code-level only — computer-use was disconnected, so populated screens need a signed-in run to eyeball.)*
*2026-06-01 update (adsense rejection fix): AdSense rejected wordocious.com for **"low value content"** — root cause was the **login wall**: only privacy/terms/support were public, so the crawler/reviewer only ever saw the login form. ### Historical snapshot (May 29, 2026)

*Preserved from the original §7 — the state of the world when the native iOS effort was mid-Phase-1. Everything below has since been superseded by the change log above (all tabs/modes complete, VS live, builds shipped from `main`).*

#### Native iOS — Phase 1 (as of May 29, 2026)

**Engine port (Phase 0 — done & verified).** Ported all of `packages/core` to Swift in `apps/ios/Sources/Core/` (Types, Evaluator, Dictionary, Seed, Scoring, Prefill, Reducer). Wrote `generate-fixtures.mjs` to emit JSON fixtures from the live TS engine, and `EngineParityTests` (XCTest) that replays them against the Swift engine — **16/16 passing**, including the critical `simpleHash` (Int32-truncated to match JS 32-bit bitwise overflow) and seed-generation cases. Confirms a seed → identical words on iOS and web.

**App scaffold (Phase 0 — done).** XcodeGen `project.yml` generates `Wordocious.xcodeproj` (bundle `com.wordocious.app`, iOS 16+) linking the `WordociousCore` SwiftPM package. The `.xcodeproj` is gitignored (regenerate via `xcodegen generate`). Verified live in the iPhone 17 Pro simulator generating that day's real daily puzzle.

**Game UI (Phase 1 — done).** `GameViewModel` drives any solo mode (1..N boards) off the pure engine. `BoardView`/`TileView` render the grid (green/yellow/gray on `#f8f7ff` lavender, matched to web); `BoardLayout` lays out 1 board full-size or 2/4/8 in a scrollable grid. `KeyboardView` is a QWERTY with per-key state coloring + native haptics. `HomeView` is a mode picker. 7 solo modes playable: Classic, Six, Seven, QuadWord, OctoWord, Sequence, Deliverance. `GamePersistence` saves/loads `GameState` per seed+mode to resume mid-game.

**Auth (Phase 1 — done, email).** `supabase-swift` integrated. `AuthService` mirrors the web `auth-context` (session restore, `authStateChanges`, profile fetch, expiry-aware `isProActive`). `Profile` model + `isProActive()` ported 1:1 from `lib/pro.ts`. `AuthView` does email sign-in/sign-up; Sign in with Apple button scaffolded (activates in Phase 2). Anon key wired and **live-verified** (REST 200; email + Google enabled on the project, Apple OAuth not yet configured).

**Code signing (done).** Apple Distribution team `Q32F6GRDYG` (same as ShowLoud) baked into `project.yml` (`DEVELOPMENT_TEAM` + automatic signing) so it survives XcodeGen regeneration. Apple recognizes it and resolves `com.wordocious.app`; simulator + distribution archive are unblocked, device builds just need a registered iPhone.

**Aesthetic match (done).** Audited the web design system and mirrored it natively: bundled the **Nunito** typeface (`UIAppFonts`), pulled exact tokens from `globals.css` (bg `#f8f7ff`, text `#1a1a2e`, border `#ede9f6`, primary `#7c3aed`), the **WORDOCIOUS** gradient wordmark (`#a78bfa→#ec4899`), the gold flame streak pill, and a 4-tab shell (`RootTabView`: Home/Leaderboard/Profile/Records) matching `bottom-nav.tsx` icons + purple tint. `ProfileTab` renders the real Supabase profile (avatar, Pro badge, level/wins/streak, medals).

**Live leaderboard (done).** `LeaderboardService` mirrors `lib/daily-service.ts` `getDailyLeaderboard()` exactly — `daily_results` with a `profiles!inner(username, avatar_url)` join, filtered by device-local day + game_mode + `play_type=solo`, ordered `composite_score desc, created_at asc`. `LeaderboardTab` has a mode picker + ranked rows. **Note:** `daily_results` SELECT policy is `TO authenticated`, so the leaderboard returns data only to signed-in users — the tab shows a sign-in prompt when signed out (verified: anon REST read returns `[]`). Records tab still a placeholder.

**Result writing (done).** `DailyScoring` ports the composite-score formula 1:1 from `lib/daily-service.ts` (`MODE_SCORE_CONFIG` + `computeScoreBreakdown`). `DailyResultsService.record` mirrors `recordDailyResult` — keyed by user+day+mode+play_type, insert-new or update-only-if-better, gated to an authenticated session (RLS). `GameViewModel` posts once when a daily game finishes (elapsed from `state.startTime`, boards solved, rows used; no-ops for non-daily/restored-finished/signed-out). So iOS daily play now produces the same rows the web does and appears on the shared leaderboard. Not yet exercised end-to-end (avoided writing test data to prod). Time is wall-clock from game start (web uses active-play time) — a minor scoring nuance to refine later.

**Records tab (done).** `RecordsService` mirrors `fetchAllTimeRecords` (`all_time_records` + `profiles!inner`, ordered by type); `RecordCatalog` ports `RECORD_LABELS` + the global/per-mode type lists + per-type value formatting from `app/records/page.tsx`. `RecordsTab` shows Global Records + Classic per-mode records as holder cards. Like the leaderboard, `all_time_records` SELECT is `TO authenticated`, so the tab is sign-in-gated. All four tabs (Home/Leaderboard/Profile/Records) now have real content.

**Gauntlet (done).** The 5-stage run (Duel→Quordle→Sequence→Rescue→Octordle, 1/4/4/4/8 boards). `GameViewModel` detects stage clears, advances via `NEXT_STAGE` (with elapsed timing), and finishes on the last stage; `GameScreen` shows the stage label + a Continue/Finish banner between stages. Gauntlet result recording computes cumulative guesses + boards-solved from `stageResults`, mirroring `gauntlet-game.tsx`. New engine test `testGauntletFullRunReachesWon` plays a complete run to WON (17/17 engine tests pass). 8 of the ~11 modes are now playable on iOS (remaining: ProperNoundle — needs its data; VS modes — Phase 3).

**Profile progression (done).** `GameResultsService` ports the core of `recordGameResult` (`lib/stats-service.ts`): `user_stats` upsert (wins/losses/games/best_score/average_time/fastest_time, same running-avg + min logic), profile `total_wins/losses`, win-streak + best, **XP** (100 win/25 loss + 50 streak + 50 daily bonus), **level** (xp/1000+1), daily-login-streak (local-day compare) + best, and the 7-day free streak-shield grant. Then calls the daily-results row and refreshes the in-memory profile. `GameViewModel` records via this on finish. **Still deferred** (fire-and-forget in web): `all_time_records` `checkAndUpdateRecord` writes (so iOS play doesn't yet *set* hall-of-records entries) and the daily "sweep" bonus.

**Visual verification (done).** Confirmed live in the iPhone 17 Pro simulator: Home (8 mode cards, gradient WORDOCIOUS wordmark, 4-tab bar), and a full Classic game loop — typed a guess, correct green/yellow/gray tile reveal, keyboard state coloring, guess counter advance. Aesthetic matches the web (Nunito, brand palette). Fixed a wordmark bug (overlay+mask → `foregroundStyle` gradient + `fixedSize`). The authenticated write loop (sign-in → play → XP/leaderboard row) is not yet E2E-tested (would require entering credentials / writing to prod).

All native work lives on branch `claude/eloquent-hodgkin-383095` (off `main` — never triggers a Vercel deploy).

## 8. Engine parity — implementation notes

The single most important invariant in this project: **the Swift, Kotlin, and TS engines must compute identically.** If they drift, daily seeds and VS matches desync between platforms.

- **The contract** is `apps/ios/Tests/Fixtures/*.json` — generated from the live TS engine by `apps/ios/generate-fixtures.mjs` and replayed by `EngineParityTests`. Covers hashing, evaluation, seed generation (5/6/7-letter), scoring, prefill, and daily-seed helpers. The Android Kotlin engine in `apps/android/core` is the same port discipline.
- **The risk line** is `simpleHash`. JavaScript bitwise ops are 32-bit; Swift `Int` is 64-bit (and Kotlin `Long` likewise). The native ports truncate to 32-bit so overflow matches exactly — this drives which words a seed selects, so an off-by-one there would diverge every puzzle. Verified bit-identical.
- **The rule:** never change game logic on one platform without porting it to the others and re-running the fixtures. Treat `packages/core` as canonical; regenerate fixtures after any change to it.
- **Dictionaries** are the same JSON word lists (`allowed`, `solutions`, and 6/7-letter variants) bundled into the web app, the iOS app bundle, and the Android app. Keep one canonical copy.

---

## 9. Build cadence

**Web/server:** commit + push to `main` → Vercel auto-deploys the web app (project `spellstrike`); the socket server auto-deploys to Railway from `main` too. The user's standing preference is auto-commit + push to `main` after every change. **Gate every web push with `pnpm -C apps/web test && pnpm -C apps/web typecheck`** — since 2026-07-17 the vitest suite carries the cross-platform drift guards (display-format fixtures, word-list 5-way sync, modes.json codegen staleness, WOTD local-day contract, hint-row reconstruction).

**Native iOS — development loop** (driven from the command line; Xcode is sandboxed to click-only for the agent):
1. Edit Swift sources / `project.yml`.
2. After adding files **or** packages, run `xcodegen generate` (regenerates the `.xcodeproj`).
3. `swift test` for the core package (engine parity + display-format fixtures + PN reconstruction — app-target code is NOT reachable by swift test; pure logic that needs tests goes in Sources/Core); `xcodebuild -scheme Wordocious -destination 'generic/platform=iOS Simulator' build` for the app.
4. `xcrun simctl install/launch` to run in the simulator.

**Native iOS — release flow** (proven across builds 2→20):
1. Bump `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION` in `project.yml` → `xcodegen generate`.
2. Archive **UNSIGNED** (`CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO`).
3. `xcodebuild -exportArchive` with **`~/.appstoreconnect/wr_export_manual.plist`** (`signingStyle=manual`, profiles *Wordocious AppStore w/ groups* + *Wordocious Widgets AppStore*) + `-allowProvisioningUpdates` + the ASC API key `C8FRS9T697` / issuer `8bdd3f73-0d8b-427d-95c7-8097b77dfb7a`. **The automatic-signing plist (`wr_export.plist`) no longer works** — it fails `No profiles for 'com.wordocious.app.widgets'` (re-confirmed on builds 122 + 123, 2026-07-16); builds 121+ all used the manual plist. Both profiles must be installed under `~/Library/MobileDevice/Provisioning Profiles/` (fetch via the ASC API if missing). The historical "manual signing → 409 Missing Provisioning Profile" note applied to manual signing with *no named profiles*, which is not this.
4. **Re-sign the exported app to restore the entitlements the export strips.** It strips **TWO**, not one (the app-groups half went undocumented until build 122, 2026-07-16):
   - app (`com.wordocious.app`): `com.apple.developer.applesignin` **and** `com.apple.security.application-groups`
   - widget (`com.wordocious.app.widgets`): `com.apple.security.application-groups`
   The widget needs the app group to read the app's shared data, so a build re-signed per the old applesignin-only recipe ships a broken widget. For each target: dump the COMPLETE exported entitlements (`codesign -d --entitlements - --xml`, which includes `application-identifier` + `team-identifier`), add the stripped keys to that full set, then `codesign -f -s E834629E… --timestamp --entitlements`. Order matters: **frameworks → .appex → .app** (signing the app last re-seals the bundle). Re-signing from a partial entitlements file drops `application-identifier` → 409.
5. `xcrun altool --validate-app` (catches entitlement/signing breakage before a wasted upload), then `--upload-app`. Poll `ruby ~/.appstoreconnect/asc_builds.rb` until the build reports `VALID`.

App Store Connect operations (attach build, cancel/resubmit review submissions, check processing) can be driven via the **ASC REST API** (ES256 JWT). Durable helper at **`~/.appstoreconnect/asc.rb`** (`builds` / `version` / `get` / `patch` / `post` / `delete`) — replaces the old `/tmp/asc_*.rb` scripts that got cleaned out. Release is always **Manual** (standing instruction). **KNOWN API LIMITATION (hit 2026-06-13; re-confirmed 2026-07-16 — still true, do not re-litigate):** the build-swap flow that works for an IN_REVIEW/WAITING version (cancel submission → PATCH `appStoreVersions/{id}/relationships/build` → POST reviewSubmission + item → submit) does **NOT** work once a version is **PENDING_DEVELOPER_RELEASE** (already approved). What *does* still work in that state: **PATCH `appStoreReviewDetails/{id}` (reviewer notes) returns 200** — notes can be updated via API without touching the web UI. The API returns `INVALID_STATE` / `STATE_NOT_SUITABLE_TO_SUBMIT` for both the build PATCH and the submission-item add, and exposes no "developer-reject" transition to return the version to an editable state. Swapping a build onto an approved-pending-release version must be done in the **ASC web UI** (which performs the implicit un-approval), or by releasing the approved build and shipping the new one as a subsequent update.

**Native Android:** test gate `./gradlew :core:test :app:testDebugUnitTest` (core fixtures + display-format/persistence/rebuild-row guards); `./gradlew assembleDebug` + emulator for development (no physical Android device — emulator-only testing); `./gradlew bundleRelease` for a **signed release AAB** (signing via the gitignored `apps/android/key.properties` → keystore `~/.android-keys/wordocious-upload.jks`).

Native work (iOS + Android) now commits to `main` alongside everything else — the web deploy is unaffected because native directories don't change web build output.

Both copies share the same GitHub remote; commit from `~/Developer`. Web deploy is unaffected (Vercel builds from GitHub `main`, not local).
- **Vercel env vars**: Sensitive — `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `CRON_SECRET`, `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` (sk_live_ from the Wordocious account), `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (the LIVE destination's whsec_ — every webhook destination mints its OWN secret; swapping destinations without re-copying it 400s every delivery), plus the Apple ASSN/verify vars. Non-secret — `STRIPE_PRICE_MONTHLY` / `STRIPE_PRICE_YEARLY` / `STRIPE_PRICE_DAY` (live price IDs, §10 Stripe bullet) + `NEXT_PUBLIC_STRIPE_ENABLED=true` (build-time inlined — env changes need a redeploy).
- **Supabase anon key**: public/publishable, shipped in the web bundle and both native apps (`SupabaseConfig.swift` / the Android config), gated by RLS
- **RLS note**: `daily_results` AND `all_time_records` SELECT are both `TO authenticated` (despite a "publicly readable" comment) — leaderboards + records require a signed-in session; anon reads return `[]`. `profiles` SELECT allows the anon REST ping (HTTP 200).
- **Cron**: `/api/cron/daily-medals` (12:05 UTC — moved from 05:05 in the audit sweep so west-of-UTC players' local-day rows finalize before podium grading; now insert-only/idempotent), `/api/cron/daily-reminder` (14:00 UTC), `/api/cron/expire-pro` (08:00 UTC daily — Pro-expiry sweep; Hobby plan allows nothing more frequent)

---

## 11. Critical risks

1. **Engine parity drift.** If the Swift, Kotlin, and TS engines diverge, cross-platform dailies and VS break. Guarded by the fixture suite — but only if it's re-run after every `packages/core` change.
2. **Android upload-keystore loss.** `~/.android-keys/wordocious-upload.jks` is the Play signing identity — losing it means losing the ability to update the app (unless Play App Signing is enrolled at submission, which it should be). **Back it up.**
3. **Pro entitlement — LOCKED (risk retired 2026-07-20, residual notes).** The `protect_pro_columns` trigger is APPLIED: regular clients can no longer write `is_pro`/`pro_expires_at`/`is_admin`/`is_banned` (service-role + admins pass; admins keep Simulate Pro; clients can't self-promote to admin). Entitlement writes now come only from verified sources: the Apple ASSN V2 webhook (REGISTERED, prod + sandbox), the Day-Pass verify endpoint (sandbox-validated end-to-end), and the Stripe webhook (live, signature-verified, validated per entry 133). RESIDUAL: the Google Play RTDN webhook is built fail-closed but unconfigured — configure it (service account + Pub/Sub) as part of Play Console setup BEFORE Android launch, since Android client grants are what the lock now blocks. Apple's revoke path (refund/expiry ASSN) has not been observed in sandbox — backstopped by the client fail-closed check + the daily expire-pro sweep. See PAYMENTS_RUNBOOK.md.
4. **VS matchmaking.** The (mode, dailySeed|random) bucketing pairs players correctly (bot matches + the VS flow are exercised regularly this cycle). A live two-human cross-platform (iOS↔web) daily-VS pairing remains the one path worth a final confirmation before public launch.
5. **AdMob Android still on Google test IDs.** Shipping to Play without creating the Android AdMob app + swapping real IDs means zero ad revenue on Android.
6. **Android Google sign-in blocked on config.** Built end-to-end, but no tokens vend until the Android OAuth client (package + SHA-1) exists in Google Cloud.
7. **ATT prompt reliability (App Review 5.1.2i).** Build 8 was rejected because the reviewer never saw the ATT prompt — it was requested at the tail of the Google UMP network chain and while the app could still be `.inactive` (iOS silently denies without showing it). FIXED in build 9+ (`AdsManager.requestATTOnce` gates on `.active` + 3s watchdog) and validated by build 10's approval. If privacy labels keep declaring tracking, every build MUST reliably present ATT on first launch or it will be re-rejected.
8. **Subscription metadata must carry an EULA link (App Review 3.1.2c).** Build 8 was also rejected for a missing Terms of Use (EULA) link in the App Store Description. The Description now includes subscription names/prices + the standard Apple EULA link + Privacy Policy — keep these whenever editing the Description.
9. **Revenue is live on web only (and iOS awaits release).** Web Stripe is LIVE on the dedicated Wordocious account (final step at write time: paste sk_live_/whsec_ → redeploy → $1 verification purchase + refund). iOS StoreKit 2 is live in the build but revenue starts only when v1.0 is released; Android Billing awaits Play Console products. Until iOS releases, wordocious.com is the only place money moves.
10. **Single-developer bus factor.** All web, native iOS, native Android, infra, and store negotiation sit with one person.
11. **iCloud eviction breaks builds (environmental).** Keeping the repo under an iCloud-synced folder (`~/Desktop`) with "Optimize Mac Storage" ON silently offloads file contents at high disk usage → all reads hang, `fileproviderd` pegs CPU. Mitigated by working from `~/Developer` (non-synced) and keeping disk < 85%. Do NOT toggle off "Desktop & Documents" sync (current macOS deletes local files with no keep-a-copy option).
12. **Apple Guideline 4.2 (minimum functionality).** Was the #1 review risk for a puzzle app; mitigated by going fully native (the lesson from ShowLoud's repeated WebView rejections) and now validated — build 10 passed App Review.
13. **AdSense web ads blocked.** wordocious.com was denied twice (low value content) — the crawler was hitting a ~7-word loading skeleton because the auth gate SSR'd the skeleton, not the public landing. Fixed 2026-07-07 (homepage + all game URLs now SSR content-rich landings; 7 strategy articles). The **site-review attempt limit resets 2026-07-14** — request review then. No web ad revenue until approved. Does NOT affect AdMob app ads. NOTE (2026-07-21): the AdSense loader script is now injected ONLY for signed-in free users (`AdSenseLoader`) — Pro users load zero ad script; this also fixed a live bug where Google's anchor-ad injection set a transform on `<html>` and detached every `position:fixed` element (the mid-page-footer report).

## 12. Pending non-engineering items

- **iOS release — final build-swap + manual release:** v1.0 is approved and held unreleased; the developer TestFlight-tests each build (**currently 127**) and swaps the blessed build onto the approved version before the manual release. **The approved v1.0 still has build 100 attached** — swapping a newer build on was attempted via API and returned 409 `INVALID_STATE` (see §9's re-confirmed limitation), so the **remaining step is UI-only**: in the ASC web UI, select the blessed build (127 as of writing) on the approved v1.0 (this implicitly un-approves it) → re-submit for review → release manually once approved. (Reviewer notes can be updated via API even in this state.) The [[reference_asc_review_swap_api]] flow (cancel→attach→submit via asc.rb) does the swap without Chrome/2FA only for a version *in review*, not an approved one.
- **AdSense re-review**: request review on the wordocious.com site in the AdSense console **on/after 2026-07-14** (site-review attempt limit reset) now that the content-rich SSR landings are live.
- **Google Play Console**: create the $25 developer account + the Wordocious app record; create the 3 billing products (`pro_monthly`, `pro_yearly`, `pro_day`); set up an internal-testing track + license testers; complete the store listing + privacy/data-safety form.
- **Android AdMob app**: create it in the AdMob console and swap the real IDs into `AndroidManifest` + `AdsManager.kt`.
- **Android OAuth client**: create in Google Cloud (package `com.wordocious.app` + the upload SHA-1) to activate native Google sign-in.
- **AdMob payments**: set up the payment profile (banking); after the iOS app is publicly released, link the App Store listing and verify `app-ads.txt`.
- **Pro entitlement enforcement**: configure + sandbox-test the App Store Server Notifications webhook, then run the deferred `lock_pro_columns` migration (see §11.3).
- **Stripe go-live, final steps**: paste the Wordocious account's `sk_live_` into `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` and the LIVE webhook destination's `whsec_` into `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (Vercel) → one redeploy → $1 Day-Pass verification purchase with a real card (confirm the webhook grants Pro) → refund it. Then: verify the statement descriptor reads WORDOCIOUS; decide on **Stripe Tax** (digital-goods sales tax/VAT registration thresholds — a business decision, launchable without it); keep the ShowLoud-account sandbox for any future test-mode work.
- **Business entity — RESOLVED**: everything ships under **ShowLoud, LLC** (Wyoming multi-member LLC; members Brian Terchin + Jasson Patterson; registered agent: Registered Agents Inc, 30 N Gould St, Sheridan WY). Payments run on the dedicated Wordocious Stripe account under that entity (§10). Open tax question: Stripe Tax / sales-tax registration thresholds as digital-subscription volume grows.
- **Keystore backup**: copy `~/.android-keys/wordocious-upload.jks` (+ `key.properties` passwords) somewhere safe off-machine.
- **Push infra for native**: APNs key (one-shot download — back it up) + FCM project, if server-pushed notifications are ever wanted (both apps currently use local/scheduled reminders).

30-day list to be locked by both founders. Summary + engineering-truth notes:

- **Part 1 (revenue on):** release iOS (build 128 is the RC pending Brian's
  device pass), finish the Android user-side checklist (§12), AdSense
  re-review, AdMob banking. All tracked in §12.
- **Part 2 (anonymous play):** the memo's premise was stale — guest play
  SHIPPED 2026-06-17 (§6a-0). The live decision is the smaller routing change:
  share/invite links should drop visitors straight onto the board (auto-guest).
- **Part 3 (organic levers):** share cards / VS invite links / SEO guides /
  ASO / launch moments — all built except two small items, now DONE

## Glossary

- **Daily seed**: `daily-YYYY-MM-DD-<MODE>` (device-local date). Deterministically selects the day's puzzle words; identical across web + iOS + Android.
- **Mode**: a puzzle variant (Classic/Quordle/Octordle/Sequence/Rescue/Gauntlet/VS/etc.), differing in board count, word length, and guess budget.
- **Gauntlet**: a single 5-stage run chaining modes; one bust ends it.
- **VS**: real-time head-to-head match on a shared seed via the socket server; matchmaking is bucketed by (mode, dailySeed|random).
- **Pro**: the single paid subscription (`pro_day` / `pro_monthly` / `pro_yearly`); entitlement = `is_pro` + `pro_expires_at`, read via `isProActive()`.
- **Streak shield**: a consumable that protects a daily-login streak; granted with monthly/yearly Pro.
- **Engine parity**: the guarantee that the Swift, Kotlin, and TS engines compute identically, enforced by JSON fixtures.
- **simpleHash**: the 32-bit string hash that drives seed→word selection; the parity linchpin.
- **PaymentProvider**: the interface/factory in `lib/payment/` that is the single swap point for web billing (now `StripeProvider`; fulfillment itself lives server-side in the Stripe webhook).
